local function scan(parent)
    for _, item in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
        -- We look for Scripts and ModuleScripts
        if item:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then 
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print("PATH: " .. item:GetFullName())
            print("TYPE: " .. item.ClassName)
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print(item.Source)
            print("\n-- END OF SCRIPT --\n")
        end
    end
end

function CreateRainDrop()
	local Attachment1 = Instance.new("Attachment")

	local CharacterPosition = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity

	local AttachmentPosition = Vector3.new(CharacterPosition.X + math.random(-60, 60), 800, CharacterPosition.Z + math.random(-60, 60))

	Attachment1.Parent = workspace.Terrain

	Attachment1.WorldPosition = AttachmentPosition

	local Attachment2 = Instance.new("Attachment")

	Attachment2.Parent = workspace.Terrain

	local RainDrop = script.Rain:Clone()

	RainDrop.Attachment0 = Attachment1

	RainDrop.Attachment1 = Attachment2

	local RaycastResult = workspace:Raycast(AttachmentPosition, CFrame.lookAt(AttachmentPosition, AttachmentPosition + Vector3.new(15, -800, 20)).LookVector * 1200, RaycastParams.new())

	Attachment2.WorldPosition = RaycastResult.Position

	RainDrop.Parent = Attachment1

	local Particles = script.RainDrop:Clone()

	Particles.Parent = Attachment2

	spawn(function()
		for Index = 1, 10 do
			wait(0.5)

			Particles:Emit(8)
		end

		Attachment2:Destroy()

		Attachment1:Destroy()
	end)

	--Debris:AddItem(Attachment1, 5)

	--Debris:AddItem(Attachment2, 5)
end

while true do
	wait(1)
	
	for i = 1, 25 do
		pcall(function()
			delay(math.random(0, 10) / 10, CreateRainDrop)
		end)

	end

local gameProcess = false
local walkKeyBinds = {
	Forward = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.W, Direction = Enum.NormalId.Front },
	Backward = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.S, Direction = Enum.NormalId.Back },
	Left = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.A, Direction = Enum.NormalId.Left },
	Right = { Key = Enum.KeyCode.D, Direction = Enum.NormalId.Right }
}

function lerp(a, b, t)
	return a + (b - a) * t
end

local function getWalkDirectionCameraSpace()
	local walkDir = Vector3.new()

	for keyBindName, keyBind in pairs(walkKeyBinds) do
		if InputS:IsKeyDown(keyBind.Key) and not InputS:GetFocusedTextBox() and not _G.meleeFinisher then
			walkDir += Vector3.FromNormalId( keyBind.Direction )
		end
	end

	if walkDir.Magnitude > 0 then --(0, 0, 0).Unit = NaN, do not want
		walkDir = walkDir.Unit --Normalize, because we (probably) changed an Axis so it's no longer a unit vector
	end

	return walkDir
end

local function getWalkDirectionWorldSpace()
	local walkDir = camera.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace( getWalkDirectionCameraSpace() )
	walkDir *= Vector3.new(1, character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and character.Humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and 1 or 0, 1) --Set Y axis to 0

	if walkDir.Magnitude > 0 then --(0, 0, 0).Unit = NaN, do not want
		walkDir = walkDir.Unit --Normalize, because we (probably) changed an Axis so it's no longer a unit vector
	end

	return walkDir
end

local targetMoveVelocity = Vector3.new()
local moveVelocity = Vector3.new()
local moveAcceleration = 8

local function updateMovement( dt )
	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid and not _G.meleeFinisher and humanoid.WalkSpeed > 0 then
		local moveDir = getWalkDirectionWorldSpace()
		if InputS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and not InputS:GetFocusedTextBox() and humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
			moveDir = Vector3.new(moveDir.x, 1, moveDir.z)
		end
		targetMoveVelocity = moveDir
		moveVelocity = lerp( moveVelocity, targetMoveVelocity, math.clamp(dt * (humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and 2.5 or moveAcceleration), 0, 1) )
		humanoid:Move( moveVelocity )
	end
end	

InputS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
	gameProcess = process
	if not process then
		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) and humanoid.HipHeight >= -0.5 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not _G.meleeFinisher then
				humanoid.Jump = true
			end
		end
	end
end)

RunS.RenderStepped:Connect(updateMovement)

local FpsCamera = {}

-- Writes a warning to the output
-- in context of the FpsCamera.

function FpsCamera:Warn(...)
	warn("[FpsCamera]", ...)
end

-- Connects a self-function by  
-- name to the provided event.

function FpsCamera:Connect(funcName, event)
	return event:Connect(function (...)
		self[funcName](self, ...)
	end)
end

-- Returns true if the client is
-- currently in first person.

function FpsCamera:IsInFirstPerson()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	
	if camera then
		if camera.CameraType.Name == "Scriptable" then
			return false
		end
		
		local focus = camera.Focus.Position
		local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
		
		return (focus - origin).Magnitude <= 1
	end
	
	return false
end

-- Returns the subject position the FpsCamera
-- wants Roblox's camera to be using right now.

function FpsCamera:GetSubjectPosition()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local subject = camera.CameraSubject

	if subject and subject:IsA("Humanoid") and subject.Health > 0 and _G.isFPS and (_G.isFPS() or _G.meleeEquipped or _G.gunEquipped or _G.emotes) then
		local character = subject.Parent
		local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then
			local cf = head.CFrame
			local offset = Vector3.new(cf.Position.x, subject.RootPart.Position.y + 1.5, cf.Position.z)
			if _G.isFPS() or _G.meleeAttack or _G.downed or _G.meleeFinisher or _G.emotes then
				offset = cf.Position
			end
			
			offset += subject.CameraOffset
			return offset, cf.LookVector
		end
	end
	
	return self:GetBaseSubjectPosition()
end

-- Overloads BaseCamera:GetSubjectPosition() with
-- the GetSubjectPosition function of the FpsCamera.

function FpsCamera:MountBaseCamera(BaseCamera)
	local base = BaseCamera.GetSubjectPosition
	self.GetBaseSubjectPosition = base
	
	if base then
		BaseCamera.GetBaseSubjectPosition = base
		BaseCamera.GetSubjectPosition = self.GetSubjectPosition
	else
		self:Warn("MountBaseCamera - Could not find BaseCamera:GetSubjectPosition()!")
	end
end

-- Called once to start the FpsCamera logic.
-- Binds and overloads everything necessary.

local started = false

function FpsCamera:Start()
	if started then
		return
	else
		started = true
	end
	
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local character = player.Character
	
	local PlayerScripts = player:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts")
	local PlayerModule = PlayerScripts:WaitForChild("PlayerModule")
	
	local baseCamera = PlayerModule:FindFirstChild("BaseCamera", true)
	if baseCamera and baseCamera:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local module = require(baseCamera)
		self:MountBaseCamera(module)
	else
		self:Warn("Start - Could not find BaseCamera module!")
	end
end

return FpsCamera

local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage = function(message: TextChatMessage)
	local props = Instance.new("TextChatMessageProperties")
	
	if message.TextSource then
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(message.TextSource.UserId)
		
		if player.gamepasses:FindFirstChild("VIP") and player.gamepasses.VIP.Value == true then
			props.PrefixText = "<font color='#fff94f'>[👑 VIP]</font> " .. message.PrefixText
		end
	end
	
	return props

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

function lerp(a, b, t)
	return a + (b - a) * t
end

local function isOccluded(v3)
	local p = RaycastParams.new()
	p.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
	p.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
	--[[local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a.WorldPosition = camera.CFrame.Position + (v3 - camera.CFrame.Position).unit * ((getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, v3) * 0.85))
	a.Visible = true
	a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	Debris:AddItem(a, 3)
	if c then
		local b = Instance.new("Part")
		b.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.Position)
		b.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		b.Transparency = 0.5
		b.Anchored = true
		b.CanCollide = false
		b.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		Debris:AddItem(b, 3)
	end]]

	return workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, (v3 - camera.CFrame.Position).unit * ((getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, v3) * 0.95)), p)
end

function removeTags(str)
	str = str:gsub("<br%s*/>", "\n")
	return (str:gsub("<[^<>]->", ""))
end

local function typewrite(label, text, increment, start)
	label.MaxVisibleGraphemes = start or 0
	label.Text = text

	for i = (start or 1), #(removeTags(text)) do
		task.wait(increment or 1 / 30)
		--_G.createSound(player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.typewrite, player.PlayerGui, nil, nil, 0.2):Play()
		label.MaxVisibleGraphemes += 1
	end
end

local function isInDoor(v3)
	local p = RaycastParams.new()
	p.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
	p.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

	local inDoor = false
	local c = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), p)
	if c and c.Instance.Size.magnitude > 10 then
		return c
	end

	return inDoor
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

_G.createSound = function(sound, parent, group, dur, pitch)
	if Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("meta", "Playing") then
		return
	end

	local newSound = sound:Clone()
	newSound.Name = "__sound"
	if typeof(parent) == "Vector3" then
		local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
		a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		a.WorldPosition = parent
		newSound.Parent = a
		Debris:AddItem(a, newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	else
		newSound.Parent = parent
		Debris:AddItem(newSound, dur or newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	end

	if pitch then
		newSound.PlaybackSpeed = sound.PlaybackSpeed + (math.random() * pitch)
	end

	if newSound.Parent:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and (newSound.Parent:IsA("BasePart") or newSound.Parent:IsA("Attachment")) then
		newSound.SoundGroup = group or game:GetService("SoundService").Game
		local emitPosition = newSound.Parent:IsA("Attachment") and newSound.Parent.WorldPosition or newSound.Parent.Position
		local result = isOccluded(emitPosition)
		if result then
			local equalizer = Storage.Miscs.Equalizer:Clone()
			equalizer.LowGain = getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, result.Position)
			equalizer.MidGain = -equalizer.LowGain
			equalizer.Parent = newSound
		--[[else
			local inDoorResult = isInDoor(emitPosition)
			if inDoorResult then
				local reverb = Storage.Miscs.Reverb:Clone()
				reverb.WetLevel = math.min(-55 + getDistance(inDoorResult.Position, emitPosition), -8)
				reverb.Parent = newSound
			end]]
		end
	end

	return newSound
end

_G.emitParticle = function(name, multiplier, v, dur)
	if not _G.onScreen(typeof(v) == "Vector3" and v or v.Position) or Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("meta", "Playing") then
		return
	end

	local n = Storage.Particles[name].Attachment:Clone()
	if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
		n.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		n.WorldPosition = v
	else
		n.Parent = v
	end
	for _,p in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
		p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") * (multiplier or 1))
	end
	Debris:AddItem(n, dur or 3)

	return n
end

local function toMaterialString(material)
	local s,_ = string.gsub(tostring(material), "Enum.Material.", "")
	return s
end

local function createMarkHole(result, part, position, scale, size, ids, color, spark)
	if not scale then
		scale = 128
	end

	local face, Width, Height, RelativeX, RelativeY = WorldToGui:WorldPositionToGuiPosition(part, position)

	local surfaceGui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
	surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * Width, scale * Height)
	surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
	surfaceGui.Face = face

	local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
	surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

	local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	decal.Image = ids[math.random(#ids)]
	decal.ImageColor3 = color
	decal.Rotation = math.random(-90, 90)
	decal.ImageTransparency = 0.2
	decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
	decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	decal.Size = UDim2.new( 0, scale * size, 0, scale * size)
	decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	decal.Position = UDim2.new(RelativeX, 0, RelativeY, 0)
	decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

	surfaceGui.Parent = part
	Debris:AddItem(surfaceGui, 30)
	--[[if math.random(2) < 2 and spark and _G.onScreen(position) and result.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
		local spark = Storage.Models.HitSparks:Clone()
		spark.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position + (result.Normal.unit * 0.05))
		spark.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		TweenService:Create(spark, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position)}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(spark, 1)
	end]]
end

function createBloodScreen(hitPos, dur)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local images = {}
	for k = 1, math.clamp(math.random(dur / 2, dur), 1, 4) do
		local image = Storage.Miscs.blood_PX:Clone()
		image.Rotation = math.random(-180, 180)
		local pos, _ = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(hitPos)
		image.Position = UDim2.new(0, pos.x + math.random(-600, 600), 0, pos.y + math.random(-600, 600))
		image.Image = blood_screen_textures[math.random(#blood_screen_textures)]
		image.ImageTransparency = 1
		image.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		TweenService:Create(image, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {ImageTransparency = math.random(15, 65) / 100, Size = UDim2.new(math.random(10, 75) / 100, 0, math.random(10, 75) / 100, 0)}):Play()
		image.Parent = player.PlayerGui.UI
		images[#images + 1] = image
		task.delay(dur or 1, function()
			TweenService:Create(image, TweenInfo.new(1), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
		end)
		Debris:AddItem(image, dur * 2)
	end

	return images
end

local decalSurfaceIndex = 0
function createGuiSurface(texture, part, pos, scale, size, config, duration)
	if typeof(size) ~= "Vector2" then
		return false
	end

	local surfaceFace, width, height, relativeX, relativeY = WorldToGui:WorldPositionToGuiPosition(part, pos)

	local surfaceGui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
	surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * width, scale * height)
	surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
	surfaceGui.ZOffset = decalSurfaceIndex
	surfaceGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
	surfaceGui.Face = surfaceFace
	decalSurfaceIndex += 1

	local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
	surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

	local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	decal.Image = texture
	decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
	decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	decal.Size = UDim2.new(0, scale * size.x, 0, scale * size.y)
	decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	decal.Position = UDim2.new(relativeX, 0, relativeY, 0)
	decal.Name = "Decal"
	for prop,data in pairs(config) do
		decal[prop] = data
	end
	decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

	surfaceGui.Parent = part
	--Debris:AddItem(surfaceGui, 10)
	task.delay(duration and duration - 1 or 9, function()
		TweenService:Create(decal, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
		task.wait(1)
		surfaceGui:Destroy()
	end)

	return surfaceGui, decal
end

_G.createGuiSurface = function(...)
	return createGuiSurface(...)
end

local function createHitParticles(cast, results)
	if not _G.onScreen(results.Position) then
		return
	end

	local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
	if results.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
		attachment.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(results.Position, results.Position + results.Normal)
	else
		attachment.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(results.Position)
	end
	attachment.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	local materialName,_ = string.gsub(tostring(results.Material), "Enum.Material.", "")
	local materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects:FindFirstChild(materialName)
	if not materialFolder then
		materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects.Concrete
	end
	for _,p in pairs(materialFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			local n = p:Clone()
			--n.Color = ColorSequence.new(results.Instance.Color)
			n.Parent = attachment
			n:Emit(n:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
		end
	end
	_G.createSound(getRandomChild(materialFolder.Sounds), attachment):Play()
	Debris:AddItem(attachment, 3)
end

function isHumanoid(part)
	if part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		return part.Parent
	elseif part.Parent ~= workspace then
		return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
	end

	return false
end

local function isObject(part)
	return part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects)
end

local function getRayPartDepth(origin, dir, part)
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {part}
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

	local rayOrigin = origin + (dir.unit * part.Size.magnitude)
	local rayDirection = origin - rayOrigin
	local cast = workspace:Raycast(rayOrigin, rayDirection, params)
	if cast then
		return getDistance(origin, cast.Position) + MaterialPenetration:getHardness(cast.Material), cast
	end

	return false
end

local function isHead(part, character)
	if string.lower(part.Name) == "head" then
		return true
	else
		local weld = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld", true)
		if weld then
			if (weld.Part0 and string.lower(weld.Part0.Name) == "head") or (weld.Part1 and string.lower(weld.Part1.Name) == "head") then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function getWeldOwnerFromPart(part, character)
	local weld = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld", true)
	if weld then
		return (weld.Part0 and part:IsDescendantOf(weld.Part0) and weld.Part0) or (weld.Part1 and part:IsDescendantOf(weld.Part1) and weld.Part1)
	elseif part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
		return character.Torso
	end

	return false
end

function getClampedVel(curVel, maxVel)
	local v3 = curVel.unit * math.min(maxVel, curVel.magnitude)
	if (string.find(tostring(v3), "nan")) then
		v3 = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
	end
	return v3
end

function hasArmor(character, limbName)
	local armorType = ArmorDatabase.LimbsToArmorType[limbName]
	if armorType and character:FindFirstChild("Armor") and character.Armor:FindFirstChild(armorType) and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Equipped") and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Health") > 0 then
		return true
	end

	return false
end

function getTableIndex(t)
	local n = 0
	for i,v in pairs(t) do
		n += 1
	end
	return n
end

function getSize(p)
	local n = 0
	n += p.Size.x
	n += p.Size.y
	n += p.Size.z
	return n
end

function debugLine(p1, p2, t, b)
	if (not config.debug and not b) then
		return
	end

	local startPoint = Storage.Miscs.rayDebug.start:Clone()
	local endPoint = Storage.Miscs.rayDebug["end"]:Clone()
	startPoint.Position = p1
	endPoint.Position = p2
	startPoint.Parent, endPoint.Parent = workspace.Ignored, workspace.Ignored
	startPoint.Beam.Attachment1 = endPoint.point
	Debris:AddItem(startPoint, t or config.default_debug_clean_time)
	Debris:AddItem(endPoint, t or config.default_debug_clean_time)
	return startPoint, endPoint
end

function normalIdFromVector(vector)
	local epsilon = 0.01
	for _,normalId in pairs(Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems()) do
		if vector.unit:Dot(Vector3.FromNormalId(normalId)) > 1 - epsilon then
			return normalId
		end
	end
end

function destroyed(x)
	if x.Parent then return false end
	local _, result = pcall(function() x.Parent = x end)
	return result:match("locked") and true or false
end

local function isBoundBy(Part1, Part2)
	return (Part1.Position.X + Part1.Size.X / 2 < Part2.Position.X + Part2.Size.X / 2) and (Part1.Position.Y + Part1.Size.Y / 2 < Part2.Position.Y + Part2.Size.Y / 2) and (Part1.Position.Z + Part1.Size.Z / 2 < Part2.Position.Z + Part2.Size.Z / 2)
end

local function blood(target, hitPart, config)
	if ReplicatedStorage.__config:WaitForChild("blood").Value == false or not _G.onScreen then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		local offset = config.trackAttachment
		if not offset then
			offset = Instance.new("Attachment")
			offset.CFrame = config.cf
			offset.Name = "_bOffset-" .. tick()
			offset.Parent = hitPart
		end

		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, camera, workspace.Characters}
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
		params.IgnoreWater = true
		params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

		local t = tick()
		for k = 1, config.amount do
			if not offset:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				break
			end

			local blood = Storage.Models.Bloods.drip:Clone()
			blood.CFrame = offset.WorldCFrame
			blood.Velocity = (config.trackAttachment and (offset.WorldCFrame[config.attachmentNormal] * config.attachmentDir) or offset.WorldCFrame.LookVector) * math.random(config.strength.min, config.strength.max) * (config.recede and tick() - t >= config.time_before_recede and config.recede - ((k / config.amount) * 1) or 1) + Vector3.new(
			math.random(config.vel.x.min, config.vel.x.max) / config.vel.x.d,
			math.random(config.vel.y.min, config.vel.y.max) / config.vel.y.d,
			math.random(config.vel.z.min, config.vel.z.max) /config.vel.z.d
			) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(config.up.min, config.up.max), 0)
			blood.Parent = workspace.Ignored

			local lastPos = blood.Position
			local connections = {}
			local function clean()
				for index,event in pairs(connections) do
					event:Disconnect()
					connections[index] = nil
				end

				connections = nil
			end

			table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local cast = workspace:Raycast(lastPos, blood.Position - lastPos, params)
				if cast then
					clean()

					if _G.onScreen(blood.Position) then
						_G.emitParticle("BloodSplatEffect", nil, blood)
					end

					pcall(function()
						local splatter
						if math.random(5) == 1 then
							splatter = getRandomChild(Storage.Models.Bloods.splatters):Clone()
							local origin = cast.Position + (cast.Normal.unit * (splatter.Size.y / 2))
							splatter.CFrame = CFrame.new(origin, origin + (cast.Normal * 2)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), math.rad(math.random(-90, 90)), 0)
							splatter.Parent = workspace.Ignored
							if not cast.Instance.Anchored then
								splatter.Anchored = false
								splatter.Massless = true
								local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
								weld.Part0 = cast.Instance
								weld.Part1 = splatter
								weld.Parent = splatter
							end

							task.delay(9, function()
								if splatter:IsDescendantOf(game) then
									TweenService:Create(splatter, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
								end
							end)

							Debris:AddItem(splatter, 10)
						end

						blood.Anchored = true
						blood.CFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position, cast.Position + cast.Normal) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), math.rad(math.random(-90, 90)), 0)
						blood.Velocity = Vector3.zero
						blood.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
						blood.Trail.Enabled = false
						if not cast.Instance.Anchored then
							blood.Anchored = false
							blood.Massless = true
							local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
							weld.Part0 = cast.Instance
							weld.Part1 = blood
							weld.Parent = blood
						end

						local size = math.random(config.size.min, config.size.max) / config.size.d
						blood.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.28, 0.1)
						blood.Transparency = 0.2
						blood:SetAttribute("Count", 0)

						task.spawn(function()
							local c = blood:GetAttribute("Count")
							local t = tick()
							while blood:IsDescendantOf(game) do
								if blood:GetAttribute("Count") > c then
									c = blood:GetAttribute("Count")
									t = tick()
								end

								if tick() - t >= 10 then
									blood:SetAttribute("Destroy", true)
									TweenService:Create(blood, TweenInfo.new(1), {Size = Vector3.zero, Transparency = 1}):Play()
									task.wait(1)
									blood:Destroy()
									break
								end

								task.wait(1 / 5)
							end
						end)

						local tween = TweenService:Create(blood, TweenInfo.new(size / 2), {Size = Vector3.new(size, 0.035, size)})
						tween:Play()
						tween.Completed:Wait()
						local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
						overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Characters, camera, workspace.Buildings, blood}
						local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(blood.CFrame, blood.Size, overlapParams)
						for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
							if isBoundBy(blood, part) then
								if not part:GetAttribute("Destroy") and typeof(part:GetAttribute("Count")) == "number" and part.Size.magnitude < 3 then
									part:SetAttribute("Count", part:GetAttribute("Count") + 1)
									local expandSize = part.Size.x + (size / 2)
									TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(expandSize / 2), {Size = Vector3.new(expandSize, 0.035, expandSize)}):Play()

									blood:Destroy()
								end

								break
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				if blood:IsDescendantOf(game) then
					lastPos = blood.Position
				end
			end))

			table.insert(connections, blood.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
				if parent == nil then
					clean()
				end
			end))

			Debris:AddItem(blood, 30)

			task.wait(math.random(config.cd[1], config.cd[2]) / config.cd[3])
		end

		if not config.trackAttachment then
			Debris:AddItem(offset, 5)
		end
	end)
end

local function createStubs(character, part)
	if ReplicatedStorage.__config:WaitForChild("blood").Value == false then
		return
	end

	if character:FindFirstChild("Torso") and not character.Torso:FindFirstChild(part.Name .. "Stubs") then
		local stubs = Storage.Models.Gibs[string.find(part.Name, "Arm") and "ArmStubs" or "LegStubs"]:Clone()
		stubs.Weld.Part0 = character.Torso
		stubs.Weld.C0 = require(stubs.Offset)[part.Name]
		stubs.Name = part.Name .. "Stubs"
		stubs.Parent = character.Torso

		if stubs:FindFirstChild("FX") then
			_G.emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, stubs.FX.WorldPosition)
			task.delay(math.random(7, 9), function()
				if stubs and stubs:FindFirstChild("FX") then
					for _,v in pairs(stubs.FX:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
							if v:GetAttribute("Rate") then
								v.Rate = v:GetAttribute("Rate")
							else
								v.Enabled = false
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		task.defer(function()
			local cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(part.CFrame.Position, part.CFrame.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * 5)))
			blood(character, part, {
				cf = cf,
				trackAttachment = stubs:FindFirstChild("FX"),
				attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
				attachmentDir = 1,
				amount = math.random(95, 100),
				cd = {1, 3, 100},
				vel = {
					x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
					y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
					z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
				},
				strength = {min = 90, max = 120},
				up = {min = 0, max = 0},
				size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
				recede = 1,
				time_before_recede = 0.65,
			})

			if part:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(part.CFrame.Position, part.CFrame.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * 5)))
				blood(character, part, {
					cf = cf,
					trackAttachment = stubs:FindFirstChild("FX"),
					attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
					attachmentDir = 1,
					amount = math.random(45, 50),
					cd = {25, 35, 100},
					vel = {
						x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
						y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
						z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
					},
					strength = {min = 30, max = 35},
					up = {min = -15, max = 5},
					size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
				})
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function gore(target, part, damage, gib, dir, position, lookVector, normal, death)
	if target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") or not part or not part:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		return
	end

	if ReplicatedStorage.__config:WaitForChild("blood").Value == false then
		return
	end

	-- // gibs system
	if part.Parent.Name == part.Name and part.Parent.Parent == target then
		part = part.Parent
	end

	if gib then
		do
			local character = player.Character
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, position) < 8 then
				createBloodScreen(position, math.min(damage / 2, 8))
			end
		end

		-- // head gib
		if (part.Name == "Head") then
			if target:FindFirstChild("Torso") and not target.Torso:FindFirstChild("TorsoStains") then
				local stains = Storage.Models.Gibs.TorsoStains:Clone()
				stains.Weld.Part0 = target.Torso
				stains.Parent = target.Torso
			end

			for _,instance in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
				if (instance.Name == "wound" or instance:IsA("SurfaceGui")) then
					instance:Destroy()
				end
			end

			if not part:GetAttribute("Dismembered") then
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.gore_ripped), part):Play()
				_G.emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)

				part.Transparency = 1
				local gibModel = getRandomChild(Storage.Models.Gibs.Head[gib]):Clone()
				gibModel.Color = part.Color
				-- // remove accessories
				for _,accessory in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
					if (accessory:IsA("Accoutrement") and accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle") and (accessory.Handle:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment") or accessory.Handle:FindFirstChild("FaceFrontAttachment") or accessory.Handle:FindFirstChild("FaceCenterAttachment") or accessory.Handle:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment"))) then						
						accessory:Destroy()
					end
				end
				--
				gibModel.Name = target.Name .. "_" .. "GIBS"
				gibModel.Parent = workspace.Ignored.Gibs
				part:SetAttribute("Dismembered", true)
				part:SetAttribute("DismemberHitCount", 0)

				if gibModel:FindFirstChild("FX") then
					task.delay(math.random(7, 9), function()
						if gibModel and gibModel:FindFirstChild("FX") then
							for _,v in pairs(gibModel.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									if v:GetAttribute("Rate") then
										v.Rate = v:GetAttribute("Rate")
									else
										v.Enabled = false
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				task.defer(function()
					local cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(part.CFrame.Position, part.CFrame.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * 5)))
					blood(target, part, {
						cf = cf,
						trackAttachment = gibModel:FindFirstChild("FX"),
						attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
						attachmentDir = 1,
						amount = math.random(95, 100),
						cd = {3, 6, 100},
						vel = {
							x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
							y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
							z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
						},
						strength = {min = 40, max = 55},
						up = {min = -15, max = 5},
						size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
						recede = 1,
						time_before_recede = 0.5,
					})

					if part:IsDescendantOf(game) then
						cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(part.CFrame.Position, part.CFrame.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * 5)))
						blood(target, part, {
							cf = cf,
							trackAttachment = gibModel:FindFirstChild("FX"),
							attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
							attachmentDir = 1,
							amount = math.random(45, 50),
							cd = {25, 35, 100},
							vel = {
								x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
								y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
								z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
							},
							strength = {min = 30, max = 35},
							up = {min = -15, max = 5},
							size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
						})
					end
				end)

				local gibModelObject = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
				gibModelObject.Name = "gibModelObject"
				gibModelObject.Value = gibModel
				gibModelObject.Parent = part
				if (gib ~= "Bottom") then
					local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
					weld.Part0 = part
					weld.Part1 = gibModel
					weld.Parent = gibModel
				elseif target:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
					local neck = Storage.Models.Gibs.Neck:Clone()
					neck.Color = part.Color
					neck.Weld.Part0 = target.Torso
					neck.Parent = gibModel
					part.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
					gibModel.CFrame = part.CFrame
					gibModel.Velocity = dir.unit
						* math.random(80, 100)
						+ Vector3.new(0, math.random(20, 30), 0)
					local totalMass = gibModel:GetMass()
					for _,part in pairs(gibModel:GetChildren()) do
						if (part:IsA("BasePart")) then
							totalMass += part:GetMass()
						end
					end
					local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
					force.Force = Vector3.new(0, totalMass * 120, 0)
					force.Parent = gibModel
					gibModel.Velocity = getClampedVel(gibModel.Velocity, 50)
				end

				local connection
				connection = target.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
					if parent == nil then
						connection:Disconnect()
						gibModel:Destroy()
					end
				end)
				local decal = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal")
				local decal1 = gibModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal")
				if (decal1 and decal) then
					decal1.Texture = decal.Texture
					decal:Destroy()
				end
			elseif part:GetAttribute("Dismembered") and part:FindFirstChild("gibModelObject") then
				if part:GetAttribute("DismemberHitCount") > 0 then
					part.gibModelObject.Value:Destroy()
					part.gibModelObject:Destroy()

					local neck = Storage.Models.Gibs.Neck:Clone()
					neck.Color = part.Color
					neck.Weld.Part0 = target.Torso
					neck.Parent = workspace.Ignored
					part.CollisionGroup = "Debris"

					local connection
					connection = target.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
						if parent == nil then
							connection:Disconnect()
							neck:Destroy()
						end
					end)

					for _,v in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
						if not v:IsA("Attachment") and not v:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end

					local sound = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.limb_breaks, part)
					if sound then
						sound:Play()
					end
				end

				part:SetAttribute("DismemberHitCount", part:GetAttribute("DismemberHitCount") + 1)
			end
		elseif (string.find(part.Name, "Arm") or string.find(part.Name, "Leg")) then
			if not part:GetAttribute("Dismembered") then
				for _,instance in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
					if (instance.Name == "wound" or instance:IsA("SurfaceGui")) then
						instance:Destroy()
					end
				end

				if target.Head:FindFirstChild("hurt") then
					for _,sound in pairs(target.Head:GetChildren()) do
						if (sound.Name == "hurt") then
							sound:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end
				if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					local sound = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.hurt), target.Head, nil, nil, 0.25)
					if sound then
						sound.Name = "hurt"
						sound:Play()
					end
				end

				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.gore_ripped), part):Play()
				_G.emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)

				part.Transparency = 1
				local gibModel = getRandomChild(Storage.Models.Gibs.Limbs[gib]):Clone()
				local clothInstance = string.find(part.Name, "Arm") and target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Shirt") or string.find(part.Name, "Leg") and target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Pants")
				for _,skin in pairs(gibModel:GetDescendants()) do
					if (skin:IsA("BasePart") and ((skin.Name == "Part" and skin.Parent.Name == "Attach") or skin.Name == "Debris")) then
						skin.Color = part.Color
						if clothInstance and skin:FindFirstChild("Shirt") then
							skin.Shirt.Texture = clothInstance:IsA("Shirt") and clothInstance.ShirtTemplate or clothInstance.PantsTemplate
						elseif skin:FindFirstChild("Shirt") then
							skin.Shirt.Texture = ""
						end
					end
				end

				local randomAngles = {-180, -90, 0, 90, 180}
				local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
				weld.Part0 = part
				weld.Part1 = gibModel.Attach
				weld.C0 *= CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(randomAngles[math.random(#randomAngles)]), 0)
				weld.Parent = gibModel

				local objectPart = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
				objectPart.Name = "LimbsGibs"
				objectPart.Value = gibModel
				objectPart.Parent = part

				gibModel.Name = target.Name .. "_" .. "GIBS"
				gibModel.Parent = workspace.Ignored.Gibs
				part:SetAttribute("Dismembered", true)
				part:SetAttribute("DismemberHitCount", 0)

				task.defer(function()
					if gibModel and gibModel:IsDescendantOf(game) and gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") then
						local cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(gibModel.Attach.Position, gibModel.Attach.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * -5)))
						blood(target, part, {
							cf = cf,
							trackAttachment = gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") and gibModel.Attach:FindFirstChild("FX"),
							attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
							attachmentDir = 1,
							amount = math.random(95, 100),
							lookVector = lookVector,
							normal = Vector3.FromNormalId(Enum.NormalId.Bottom),
							cd = {1, 3, 100},
							vel = {
								x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
								y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
								z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
							},
							strength = {min = 90, max = 120},
							up = {min = 0, max = 0},
							size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
							recede = 1,
							time_before_recede = 0.65,
						})

						if part:IsDescendantOf(game) and gibModel and gibModel:IsDescendantOf(game) and gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") then
							cf = part.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(gibModel.Attach.Position, gibModel.Attach.Position + (part.CFrame.UpVector * -5)))
							blood(target, part, {
								cf = cf,
								trackAttachment = gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") and gibModel.Attach:FindFirstChild("FX"),
								attachmentNormal = "UpVector",
								attachmentDir = 1,
								amount = math.random(45, 50),
								lookVector = lookVector,
								normal = Vector3.FromNormalId(Enum.NormalId.Bottom),
								cd = {25, 35, 100},
								vel = {
									x = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
									y = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100},
									z = {min = -500, max = 500, d = 100}
								},
								strength = {min = 30, max = 35},
								up = {min = -15, max = 5},
								size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100},
							})
						end
					end
				end)

				if gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") and gibModel.Attach:FindFirstChild("FX") then
					task.delay(math.random(7, 9), function()
						if gibModel:FindFirstChild("Attach") and gibModel.Attach:FindFirstChild("FX") then
							for _,v in pairs(gibModel.Attach.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									if v:GetAttribute("Rate") then
										v.Rate = v:GetAttribute("Rate")
									else
										v.Enabled = false
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				local debrisWeld = gibModel.Attach:FindFirstChild("Debris")
				if debrisWeld then 
					task.delay(1 / 30, function()
						debrisWeld:Destroy()

						if _G.onScreen and _G.onScreen(part.Position) then
							gibModel.Debris.Velocity = (dir.unit * math.random(30, 50)) + Vector3.new(
							math.random(-100, 100) / 100,
							math.random(-100, 100) / 100,
							math.random(-100, 100) / 100
							)
							gibModel.Debris.RotVelocity = gibModel.Debris.Velocity
							local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
							force.Force = Vector3.new(0, gibModel.Debris:GetMass() * 120, 0)
							force.Parent = gibModel.Debris

							Debris:AddItem(gibModel.Debris, 8)
						else
							gibModel.Debris:Destroy()
						end
					end)
				end

				local connection
				connection = target.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
					if parent == nil then
						connection:Disconnect()
						gibModel:Destroy()
						objectPart:Destroy()
					end
				end)
			else
				if part:GetAttribute("DismemberHitCount") > 0 then
					for _,v in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Name == "LimbsGibs" and v.Value then
							v.Value:Destroy()
							v:Destroy()
							part.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
							createStubs(target, part)
						end
					end

					local sound = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.limb_breaks, part)
					if sound then
						sound:Play()
					end
				end

				part:SetAttribute("DismemberHitCount", part:GetAttribute("DismemberHitCount") + 1)
			end
		end
	end
end

local function recoil(a, b, c)
	local d = 1 / (c and 1)
	return math.random(a * d, b * d) / d
end

local function reflect(surfaceNormal, bulletNormal)
	return bulletNormal - (2 * bulletNormal:Dot(surfaceNormal) * surfaceNormal)
end

local function isObstacle(p1, p2, params)
	return workspace:Raycast((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1), (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2) - (typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1), params)
end

local function newBeam(w0)
	local ptos = CFrame.new().pointToObjectSpace
	local vtws = CFrame.new().vectorToWorldSpace
	local t0, p0, v0
	local t1, p1, v1 = Clock:GetTime(), camera.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(w0)
	local attach0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	local attach1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
	local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
	beam.Attachment0 = attach0
	beam.Attachment1 = attach1
	beam.Enabled = true
	beam.Segments = 16
	beam.TextureSpeed = 0
	beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
	beam.FaceCamera = true
	attach0.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	attach1.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	beam.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	local function update(w2, t2, size, bloom, brightness)
		local t2 = Clock:GetTime()
		local p2 = camera.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(w2)
		local v2
		if t0 then
			v2 = 2 / (t2 - t1) * (p2 - p1) - (p2 - p0) / (t2 - t0)
		else
			v2 = (p2 - p1) / (t2 - t1)
			v1 = v2
		end
		t0, v0, p0 = t1, v1, p1
		t1, v1, p1 = t2, v2, p2
		local dt = t1 - t0
		local m0 = v0.magnitude
		local m1 = v1.magnitude
		beam.CurveSize0 = dt / 3 * m0
		beam.CurveSize1 = dt / 3 * m1
		attach0.Position = camera.CFrame * p0
		attach1.Position = camera.CFrame * p1
		if m0 > 1.0E-8 then
			attach0.Axis = vtws(camera.CFrame, v0 / m0)
		end
		if m1 > 1.0E-8 then
			attach1.Axis = vtws(camera.CFrame, v1 / m1)
		end
		local dist0 = -p0.z
		local dist1 = -p1.z
		if dist0 < 0 then
			dist0 = 0
		end
		if dist1 < 0 then
			dist1 = 0
		end
		local w0 = size + bloom * dist0
		local w1 = size + bloom * dist1
		local l = ((p1 - p0)*Vector3.new(1, 1, 0)).magnitude
		local tr = 1 - 4 * size * size / ((w0 + w1) * (2 * l + w0 + w1)) * brightness
		beam.Width0 = w0
		beam.Width1 = w1
		beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(tr)
	end

	local function remove()
		attach0:Destroy()
		attach1:Destroy()
		beam:Destroy()
	end

	return beam, update, remove
end

local hitmarkTween
local function fire(toolName, origin, dir, character, handle)
	if Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("meta", "Playing") then
		return
	end

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[toolName])

	local isPlayerClient = character == player.Character
	if isPlayerClient then
		if camera:FindFirstChild("__blurGunFire") then camera.__blurGunFire:Destroy() end
		local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
		blur.Size = 10
		blur.Name = "__blurGunFire"
		blur.Parent = camera
		TweenService:Create(blur, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = 0}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(blur, 1.5)

		SP_recoil.recoil.x.speed(settings.recoil.RecoilSpeed)
		SP_recoil.recoil.y.speed(settings.recoil.RecoilSpeed)
		SP_recoil.recoil.z.speed(settings.recoil.RecoilSpeed)
		local recoilReduce = _G.aim and settings.recoilReduce or 1
		local x = math.rad(settings.recoil.Recoil * recoil(settings.recoil.AngleX_Min, settings.recoil.AngleX_Max, settings.recoil.Accuracy) * (settings.mode == 2 and 0.5 or 1)) * recoilReduce
		local y = math.rad(settings.recoil.Recoil * recoil(settings.recoil.AngleY_Min, settings.recoil.AngleY_Max, settings.recoil.Accuracy) * (settings.mode == 2 and 0.5 or 1)) * recoilReduce
		local z = math.rad(settings.recoil.Recoil * recoil(settings.recoil.AngleZ_Min, settings.recoil.AngleZ_Max, settings.recoil.Accuracy) * (settings.mode == 2 and 0.5 or 1)) * recoilReduce
		SP_recoil:accelerate(x, y, z)

		task.delay(1 / 30, function()
			local recoilBack = _G.isCrouch and 0.98 or _G.isProne and 1 or 0.95
			SP_recoil:accelerate(-x * recoilBack, -y * recoilBack, 0)
		end)
	end
	local dist = getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, handle)
	local tool = handle.Parent.Parent
	local toolData = tool:WaitForChild("_data")
	local halfMagSize = 10
	local currentMagOfHalfMaxSize = math.min(toolData.magCurrent.Value, halfMagSize)
	local pitch = 0.5 * (1 - currentMagOfHalfMaxSize / halfMagSize)
	if handle.MuzzleFX:FindFirstChild("FireSound") then
		local firesound = _G.createSound(handle.MuzzleFX.FireSound, handle.MuzzleFX, SoundService.Game.Gunshots)
		firesound.PlaybackSpeed += pitch
		firesound.RollOffMinDistance = math.max(firesound.RollOffMinDistance / (isPlayerClient and 6 or 2), 0.5)
		if isPlayerClient then
			firesound.Volume = firesound.Volume * (_G.isFPS() and 0.8 or 1)
		end
		firesound:Play()
		if pitch > 0 then
			local emptysound = _G.createSound(handle.MuzzleFX.Empty, handle.MuzzleFX)
			emptysound.PlaybackSpeed += pitch
			emptysound:Play()
		end
	end
	if handle.MuzzleFX:FindFirstChild("Spark") then
		local spark = handle.MuzzleFX.Spark:Clone()
		spark.Name = "_" .. spark.Name
		spark.Enabled = true
		spark.Parent = handle.MuzzleFX
		Debris:AddItem(spark, 0.05)
	end

	for _,v in pairs(handle.MuzzleFX:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and not v:GetAttribute("NoEmit") then
			v:Emit(character == player.Character and _G.aim and _G.isFPS() and v:GetAttribute("EmitCount") / 2 or v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
		end
	end
	if settings.gunType == "rpg" and handle:FindFirstChild("BackMuzzleFX") then
		for _,v in pairs(handle.BackMuzzleFX:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
				v:Emit(character == player.Character and _G.aim and _G.isFPS() and not settings.ignoreAim and v:GetAttribute("EmitCount") / 2 or v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
			end
		end
	elseif settings.gunType == "diy_ft" and player.Character == character then
		local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
		overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored}
		overlapParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

		local hitted = {}
		local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(handle.Parent.Hitbox.CFrame, handle.Parent.Hitbox.Size, overlapParams)
		for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
			local target = isHumanoid(part)
			if target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and not hitted[target] and part.Parent == target and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
				hitted[target] = true

				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, target, character}
				params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
				if not isObstacle(character.Head, part, params) then
					local UI = player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")
					task.spawn(function()
						local state, isHeadshot, isDead, errorData = Network:InvokeServer("damage", target, tool, part, dir, nil, dir)
						if state then
							if hitmarkTween then
								hitmarkTween:Cancel()
							end
							UI.hitmark.Rotation = math.random(-350, 350) / 10
							UI.hitmark.UIScale.Scale = math.random(200, 250) / 100
							UI.hitmark.ImageTransparency = 0
							UI.hitmark.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
							TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Scale = 1}):Play()
							TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Rotation = 0}):Play()
							hitmarkTween = TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.1), {ImageTransparency = 1, ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
							hitmarkTween:Play()
							Storage.Sounds.hitmark:Play()

							local hitPart = getWeldOwnerFromPart(part, target)
							local hitMark = Storage.Miscs.hitMark:Clone()
							hitMark.Parent = hitPart or part
							TweenService:Create(hitMark, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}):Play()
							Debris:AddItem(hitMark, 1.5)
						else
							if errorData then
								_G.messageLog(errorData.prop, errorData.err, errorData.dur)
							end
							player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.event_error:Play()
						end
					end)
				end
			elseif not target and part.Material == Enum.Material.Glass and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
				Network:FireServer("vars", "breakGlass", {glass = part, t = tool})
			end
		end

		table.clear(hitted)
		hitted = nil
	end
	if handle:FindFirstChild("shellPos") and not settings.ejectSniper and dist < 75 and (_G.onScreen(handle.Position) or character == player.Character) then
		task.defer(function()
			local bullet = Storage.Models.Cartridges[settings.cartridge]:Clone()
			bullet.Parent = workspace.Ignored
			if bullet:FindFirstChild("Bullet") then
				bullet.Bullet.Transparency = 1
			end
			bullet.CFrame = handle.shellPos.WorldCFrame * (settings.ejectCFrame or CFrame.new())
			bullet.Velocity = handle.shellPos.WorldCFrame.LookVector 
				* math.random(settings.ejectForce.x[1], settings.ejectForce.x[2]) 
				+ Vector3.new(0, math.random(settings.ejectForce.y[1], settings.ejectForce.y[2]), 0)
			bullet.RotVelocity = bullet.Velocity * 2
			bullet.smoke.Enabled = true

			local connection
			connection = bullet.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
				if not hit:IsDescendantOf(character) and not hit:IsDescendantOf(camera) then
					connection:Disconnect()
					_G.createSound(getRandomChild(bullet.Name == "12g" and Storage.Sounds.casings_plastic or Storage.Sounds.casings), bullet):Play()

					task.wait(math.random(5, 20) / 100)
					if bullet:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
						bullet.smoke.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end)
			Debris:AddItem(bullet, 3)
		end)
	end

	if settings.gunType == "rpg" or settings.gunType == "noFire" or settings.noFire then
		return
	end

	local spreadReduce = ((_G.aim and character == player.Character and (settings.spreadRequireFPS and _G.isFPS() or not settings.spreadRequireFPS) or character ~= player.Character and character.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Aim")) and settings.spreadReduce or 1)
	if not tool:GetAttribute("RateHeat") or tick() - tool:GetAttribute("LastFired") >= settings.rate * 2 then
		tool:SetAttribute("RateHeat", (settings.spreadRequireFPS or settings.shotgun) and 1 or 0)
	elseif not settings.spreadRequireFPS and not settings.shotgun then
		tool:SetAttribute("RateHeat", lerp(tool:GetAttribute("RateHeat"), 1, 0.1))
	end
	tool:SetAttribute("LastFired", tick())

	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, character, camera}
	behavior.RaycastParams = params
	local function rayPierce(cast, result, segmentVel)
		local depth, depthResult = getRayPartDepth(result.Position, cast.UserData["dir"], result.Instance)
		if result.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water and depth and depth <= settings.penetrate_depth and not isHumanoid(result.Instance) and cast.UserData["pierceDamage"] < settings.damage[2] / 20 then
			local partBreak = false
			if result.Instance.Material == Enum.Material.Glass and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
				partBreak = true
				if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character) == player then
					Network:FireServer("vars", "breakGlass", {glass = result.Instance, t = tool})
				end
			end

			local holeTable = bulletHoles[toMaterialString(result.Material)] or bulletHoles.Concrete
			local size = math.random(cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[1], cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[2]) / cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[3]
			if not partBreak then
				createMarkHole(result,
					result.Instance,
					result.Position,
					128,
					size + holeTable.size,
					holeTable.textures,
					Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
					true
				)
			end
			createHitParticles(cast, result)

			if not partBreak then
				createMarkHole(depthResult,
					depthResult.Instance,
					depthResult.Position,
					128,
					size + holeTable.size,
					holeTable.textures,
					Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
					true
				)

				if cast.UserData["prevPierce"] then
					local tracerPenetrate = Storage.Miscs.tracerPart:Clone()
					tracerPenetrate.CFrame = CFrame.new(cast.UserData["prevPierce"])
					tracerPenetrate.Parent = workspace.Ignored
					tracerPenetrate.Trail.Lifetime = 1.5
					tracerPenetrate.Trail.Enabled = true
					Debris:AddItem(tracerPenetrate, 3)
					task.delay(1 / 30, function()
						tracerPenetrate.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position)
					end)
				end
			end

			cast.UserData["prevPierce"] = depthResult.Position

			cast.UserData["pierceDamage"] += depth + MaterialPenetration:getHardness(result.Material)

			return true
		elseif result.Material == Enum.Material.Water then
			createHitParticles(cast, result)
			return true
		elseif cast.UserData["ric"] < 2 and not isHumanoid(result.Instance) then
			if segmentVel.unit:Dot(result.Normal) < -0.45 then
				return false
			end
			cast.UserData["ric"] += 1

			local position = result.Position
			local normal = result.Normal

			local newNormal = reflect(normal, segmentVel.unit)
			cast:SetVelocity(newNormal * (segmentVel.magnitude / 1.5))
			cast:SetAcceleration(behavior.Acceleration * 1.5)

			cast:SetPosition(position)

			return true
		end

		return false
	end
	behavior.CanPierceFunction = rayPierce

	for k = 1, settings.amountPerRound do
		local spreadness = Vector3.new(
			math.random(settings.spread.x[1], settings.spread.x[2]) / settings.spread.x[3],
			math.random(settings.spread.y[1], settings.spread.y[2]) / settings.spread.y[3],
			math.random(settings.spread.z[1], settings.spread.z[2]) / settings.spread.z[3]
		) * tool:GetAttribute("RateHeat") * spreadReduce
		local direction = dir + spreadness	
		local cast = raycast:Fire(
			origin,
			direction,
			settings.vel,
			behavior
		)

		cast.UserData["pierceDamage"] = 0
		cast.UserData["ric"] = 0
		cast.UserData["hit"] = false
		cast.UserData["whizzled"] = false
		cast.UserData["dir"] = direction
		cast.UserData["baseDir"] = dir
		cast.UserData["character"] = character
		cast.UserData["lookVector"] = character.Head.CFrame.LookVector
		cast.UserData["from"] = character.Head.Position
		cast.UserData["handle"] = handle
		cast.UserData["tool"] = character:FindFirstChild(toolName)
		cast.UserData["settings"] = settings

		local position = handle.MuzzleFX.WorldCFrame.p
		local velocity = direction * settings.vel
		local bullet, bulletupdate, bulletremove = newBeam(handle.MuzzleFX.WorldCFrame.p, {true, Vector3.new(), direction})
		bullet.Texture = "rbxassetid://2650195052"
		bullet.LightEmission = 1
		if settings.tracer_color then
			bullet.Color = settings.tracer_color
		else
			bullet.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 149, 78))	
		end

		local rateTick = 0
		local connection
		connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			local isHit = false
			local hit,_ = pcall(function()
				isHit = cast.UserData["hit"]
			end)
			if hit and not isHit then
				local position0 = position
				local velocity0 = velocity
				local dposition = dt * velocity0 + dt * dt / 2 * (Vector3.new(0, -workspace.Gravity, 0))
				position = position0 + dposition

				if tick() - rateTick >= 1 / 30 then
					rateTick = tick()
					local time = Clock:GetTime()
					bulletupdate(cast.UserData["ric"] > 0 and cast.UserData.lastPoint or position, time, 0.25, 0.005, 400)
				end
			else
				connection:Disconnect()
				bulletremove()
			end
		end)

		_G.clientDebugStats.threads += 1
	end
end

raycast.LengthChanged:Connect(function(cast, lastPoint, dir, point, segmentVel, _)
	local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local lastDistanceFromHead = head and cast.UserData.lastPoint and getDistance(cast.UserData.lastPoint, head.CFrame.Position)
	local distanceFromHead = head and getDistance(lastPoint, head.CFrame.Position)
	local distanceFromShooter = head and getDistance(cast.UserData["from"], head.CFrame.Position)
	if lastDistanceFromHead and distanceFromHead and distanceFromShooter and distanceFromHead < 20 and lastDistanceFromHead - distanceFromHead > 0 and cast.UserData.character ~= player.Character and not cast.UserData.whizzled then
		cast.UserData.whizzled = true
		local canWhizz = not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(cast.UserData.character) or ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[cast.UserData.character.Name].pvp.Value == true
		if canWhizz and (ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == true or not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(cast.UserData.character)) then
			if distanceFromShooter < 250 and player.Character and player.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
				blur.Size = 20
				blur.Parent = camera
				TweenService:Create(blur, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = 0}):Play()
				Debris:AddItem(blur, 1.5)

				local x = math.rad((cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Recoil * recoil(cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleX_Min, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleX_Max, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Accuracy) * 0.25) / cast.UserData["settings"].amountPerRound)
				local y = math.rad((cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Recoil * recoil(cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleY_Min, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleY_Max, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Accuracy) * 0.25) / cast.UserData["settings"].amountPerRound)
				local z = math.rad((cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Recoil * recoil(cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleZ_Min, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.AngleZ_Max, cast.UserData["settings"].recoil.Accuracy) * 0.5) / cast.UserData["settings"].amountPerRound)
				SP_recoil:accelerate(x, y, z)
				task.delay(1 / 30, function()
					SP_recoil:accelerate(-x, -y, 0)
				end)

				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.whizz.close), player.PlayerGui):Play()

				local UI = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("UI")
				if UI then
					UI.vignette_hit.ImageTransparency = 0.5
					TweenService:Create(UI.vignette_hit, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
				end
			else
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.whizz.far), player.PlayerGui):Play()
			end
		end
	end

	cast.UserData.lastPoint = lastPoint
end)

raycast.RayHit:Connect(function(cast, result, segmentVel, _)
	if cast.UserData["prevPierce"] then
		local tracerPenetrate = Storage.Miscs.tracerPart:Clone()
		tracerPenetrate.CFrame = CFrame.new(cast.UserData["prevPierce"])
		tracerPenetrate.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		tracerPenetrate.Trail.Lifetime = 1.5
		tracerPenetrate.Trail.Enabled = true
		Debris:AddItem(tracerPenetrate, 3)
		task.delay(1 / 30, function()
			tracerPenetrate.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position)
		end)
	end
	cast.UserData.hit = true
	local target = isHumanoid(result.Instance)
	if target and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") --[[and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)]] then
		local instance = result.Instance
		if result.Instance.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and result.Instance.Parent:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
			instance = result.Instance.Parent.Torso
		end
		local headshot = isHead(instance, target)
		local hasArmor = hasArmor(target, instance.Name)
		if cast.UserData.character == player.Character then
			local UI = player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")
			task.spawn(function()
				local state, isHeadshot, isDead, errorData = Network:InvokeServer("damage", target, cast.UserData["tool"], result.Instance, segmentVel, {
					Instance = instance,
					Position = result.Position,
					Normal = result.Normal
				}, cast.UserData["dir"])
				if state then
					if hitmarkTween then
						hitmarkTween:Cancel()
					end
					UI.hitmark.Rotation = math.random(-350, 350) / 10
					UI.hitmark.UIScale.Scale = math.random(150, 200) / 100
					UI.hitmark.ImageTransparency = 0
					UI.hitmark.ImageColor3 = headshot and Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Scale = 1}):Play()
					TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Rotation = 0}):Play()
					hitmarkTween = TweenService:Create(UI.hitmark, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.5), {ImageTransparency = 1, ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)})
					hitmarkTween:Play()
					if headshot then
						--_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.hs_mark, player.PlayerGui):Play()
						Storage.Sounds.hs_mark:Play()
					else
						Storage.Sounds.hitmark:Play()
						--_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.hitmark, player.PlayerGui):Play()
					end

					local hitPart = getWeldOwnerFromPart(instance, target)
					local hitMark = Storage.Miscs.hitMark:Clone()
					hitMark.Parent = hitPart or instance
					TweenService:Create(hitMark, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}):Play()
					Debris:AddItem(hitMark, 1.5)
				else
					if errorData then
						_G.messageLog(errorData.prop, errorData.err, errorData.dur)
					end
					player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.event_error:Play()
				end
			end)
		end	
		if not isObject(target) then
			if headshot then
				_G.createSound(hasArmor and getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.helmet_hit) or getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), result.Position):Play()
			else
				_G.createSound(hasArmor and getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.kevlar_hit) or getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHeadshotHits), result.Position):Play()
			end
			if not hasArmor then
				if not instance:GetAttribute("Dismembered") then
					createMarkHole(result,
						instance,
						result.Position,
						128,
						math.random(cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[1] * 2, cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[2] * 2) / cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[3],
						fleshHitIcons,
						Color3.fromRGB(215, 0, 0)
					)
				end

				if ReplicatedStorage.__config.blood.Value == true then
					_G.emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", headshot and 2, result.Position)
				end
			else
				--_G.emitParticle("ArmorHitEffect", nil, result.Position)
				createHitParticles(cast, {
					Instance = instance,
					Material = result.Material,
					Position = result.Position,
					Normal = result.Normal
				})
			end
		else
			local holeTable = bulletHoles[toMaterialString(result.Material)] or bulletHoles.Concrete
			local size = math.random(cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[1], cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[2]) / cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[3]
			createMarkHole(result,
				instance,
				result.Position,
				128,
				size + holeTable.size,
				holeTable.textures,
				Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
				true
			)
			createHitParticles(cast, result)
		end
		--createBlood(cast, result, segmentVel)
	elseif not target then
		local holeTable = bulletHoles[toMaterialString(result.Material)] or bulletHoles.Concrete
		local size = math.random(cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[1], cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[2]) / cast.UserData["settings"].bulletHoleSize[3]
		createMarkHole(result,
			result.Instance,
			result.Position,
			128,
			size + holeTable.size,
			holeTable.textures,
			Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
			true
		)
		createHitParticles(cast, result)
	end

	for index,instance in pairs(cast.UserData) do
		if typeof(instance) == "Instance" and (instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Terrain) or instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Ignored)) then
			Debris:AddItem(instance, index == "tracerPart" and 1.5 or 0.05)
		end
		cast.UserData[index] = nil
	end
end)

raycast.CastTerminating:Connect(function(cast)
	if not cast.UserData.hit then
		for index,instance in pairs(cast.UserData) do
			if typeof(instance) == "Instance" and (instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Terrain) or instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Ignored)) then
				instance:Destroy()
			end
			cast.UserData[index] = nil
		end
	end

	_G.clientDebugStats.threads = math.max(_G.clientDebugStats.threads - 1, 0)
end)

local function updates(character, data)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid.RootPart and _G.onScreen and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") and character.Torso:FindFirstChild("Right Shoulder") and character.Torso:FindFirstChild("Left Shoulder") and character.Torso:FindFirstChild("Neck") then
		local rightShoulder = character.Torso["Right Shoulder"]
		local leftShoulder = character.Torso["Left Shoulder"]

		local neck = character.Torso.Neck
		local originNeckC0 = CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)

		if character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") then
			if data.mouseOrigin and data.mouseDir then
				local armOffset = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and (data.move == true and data.walkSpeed > 0 and 0 or -1) or data.prone and 1.5 or 0
				if rightShoulder then
					local c0 = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(armOffset + -math.asin((data.mouseOrigin - data.mouseDir).unit.y), 1.55, 0)
					if _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) then
						TweenService:Create(rightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {C0 = c0}):Play()
					else
						rightShoulder.C0 = c0
					end
				end
				if leftShoulder then
					local c0 = CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(armOffset + -math.asin((data.mouseOrigin - data.mouseDir).unit.y), -1.55, 0)
					if _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) then
						TweenService:Create(leftShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {C0 = c0}):Play()
					else
						leftShoulder.C0 = c0
					end
				end
			end
		else
			if rightShoulder then
				local c0 = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 1.55, 0)
				if _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) then
					TweenService:Create(rightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = c0}):Play()
				else
					rightShoulder.C0 = c0
				end
			end
			if leftShoulder then
				local c0 = CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -1.55, 0)
				if _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) then
					TweenService:Create(leftShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = c0}):Play()
				else
					leftShoulder.C0 = c0
				end
			end
		end

		local cameraDirection = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame:toObjectSpace(data.cameraCF).lookVector
		if neck then
			local c0 = CFrame.new(0, originNeckC0.y, 0) 
				* CFrame.Angles(3 * math.pi / 2, 0, math.pi) 
				* CFrame.Angles(0, 0, -math.asin(cameraDirection.x)) 
				* CFrame.Angles(-math.asin(cameraDirection.y), 0, 0)
				* CFrame.Angles(0, humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and 0 or math.rad(data.neckAngle), 0)
			if _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) then
				TweenService:Create(neck, TweenInfo.new(0.35), {C0 = c0}):Play()
			else
				neck.C0 = c0
			end
		end

		if data.aim ~= nil then
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Aim", data.aim)
		end

		if data.crouch ~= nil then
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Crouch", data.crouch)
		end

		if data.prone ~= nil then
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Prone", data.prone)
		end
	end
end

function randomXp(Person: StringValue)
	local Characters = workspace:WaitForChild("Characters")
	local FindCharacter = Characters:FindFirstChild(Person)

	local PlayerConfiguration = nil

	local xpAmounts = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage"):WaitForChild("Modules").xpAmounts)	

	if FindCharacter and FindCharacter:FindFirstChild("IsDummy") then
		PlayerConfiguration = false
	else
		PlayerConfiguration = true
	end

	if PlayerConfiguration == true then
		return math.random(xpAmounts.Player.min, xpAmounts.Player.max) + math.floor(xpAmounts.Player.wanted)
	else
		return math.random(xpAmounts.Dummy.min, xpAmounts.Dummy.max)
	end			
end


local function killFX(targetName, weaponName)	
	local UI = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("UI")
	if UI then

		local color = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		color.Brightness = 0.05
		color.Saturation = -0.5
		color.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(251, 255, 255)
		color.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
		TweenService:Create(color, TweenInfo.new(0.75, Enum.EasingStyle.Circular), {Brightness = 0, Saturation = 0, TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(color, 1)
		player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.killed.TimePosition = 0
		player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.killed:Play()

		local newText = Storage.Miscs.killfeedText:Clone()
		newText.Text = ""
		newText.Parent = UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.killFeed
		local sizeY = newText.Size.Y.Offset
		newText.Size = UDim2.new(0, newText.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)
		TweenService:Create(newText, TweenInfo.new(0.35), {Size = UDim2.new(0, newText.Size.X.Offset, 0, sizeY)}):Play()
		local xp = randomXp(targetName)
		local exp = "[+" .. xp .. " EXP]"
		Storage.Events.xpGain:FireServer(xp)
		Storage.Events.wantedGain:FireServer(targetName)		
		typewrite(newText, [[<font color="rgb(255, 236, 68)"><font size="17">]] .. exp .. [[</font></font> ]] .. [[Enemy Killed!]], 1 / 45)
		task.wait(1)
		typewrite(newText, [[<font color="rgb(255, 236, 68)"><font size="17">]] .. exp .. [[</font></font> ]] .. [[<font color="rgb(155, 0, 0)">]] .. targetName .. [[</font> ]] .. "[" .. (weaponName or "N/A") .. "]", 1 / 40, #exp)
		task.wait(3)
		TweenService:Create(newText, TweenInfo.new(0.75), {TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, newText.Size.X.Offset, 0, 0)}):Play()
		task.wait(0.75)
		newText:Destroy()
	end
end

local function clients(type, ...)
	if type == "createMarkHole" then
		createMarkHole(...)
	elseif type == "createHitParticles" then
		createHitParticles(...)
	elseif type == "fire" then
		fire(...)
	elseif type == "updates" then
		updates(...)
	elseif type == "createSound" then
		local sound = _G.createSound(...)
		if sound then
			sound:Play()
		end
	elseif type == "gore" then
		local args = {...}
		task.defer(function()
			gore(unpack(args))
		end)
	elseif type == "blood" then
		local args = {...}
		task.defer(function()
			blood(unpack(args))
		end)
	elseif type == "killFX" then
		killFX(...)
	elseif type == "glass" then
		local part, transparent, hitPos, velDir = unpack({...})
		for _,v in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end

		if _G.onScreen and _G.onScreen(hitPos) then
			Shatter:Shatter(part, transparent, hitPos, velDir)
		end
	elseif type == "projectile_debris" then
		local name, pos, normal, lastPos = unpack({...})
		local debris = Storage.Models.Projectiles_Debris:FindFirstChild(name)
		if debris then
			local debrisModel = debris:Clone()
			debrisModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(pos, normal))
			debrisModel.Parent = workspace.Ignored
			for _,v in pairs(debrisModel:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") then
					local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
					force.Force = Vector3.new(0, v:GetMass() * 120, 0)
					force.Parent = v
					v.Anchored = false
					v.Velocity = normal * math.random(5, 15)
						+ Vector3.new(
							math.random(-100, 100) / 5,
							math.random(50, 150) / 10,
							math.random(-100, 100) / 5
						)
				end
			end
			Debris:AddItem(debrisModel, 5)
		end
	elseif type == "grenade_impact" then
		local model = unpack({...})
		local connections = {}
		local lastVel = model.PrimaryPart.Velocity
		local landDist = Vector3.new()
		local function clean()
			for index,event in pairs(connections) do
				event:Disconnect()
				connections[index] = nil
			end

			connections = nil
		end

		table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not model.PrimaryPart or not model.PrimaryPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				clean()
				return
			end

			local cast = workspace:Raycast(model.PrimaryPart.Position, model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.unit * 10 + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0), params)
			if lastVel.magnitude - model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.magnitude > 2.5 and getDistance(model.PrimaryPart, landDist) > 2.5 and cast then
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.grenade_bounce), model.PrimaryPart):Play()
				landDist = model.PrimaryPart.Position
			end

			lastVel = model.PrimaryPart.Velocity
		end))

		table.insert(connections, model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
			if parent == nil then
				clean()
			end
		end))
	elseif type == "grenade_debris" then
		local pos = unpack({...})
		local debrisModel = Storage.Models.Debris.Explosion:Clone()
		debrisModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(pos))
		debrisModel.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		for _,v in pairs(debrisModel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("BasePart") then
				local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
				force.Force = Vector3.new(0, v:GetMass() * 120, 0)
				force.Parent = v
				v.Anchored = false
				v.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, math.random(20, 40), 0)
					+ Vector3.new(
						math.random(-50, 50) / 5,
						math.random(50, 150) / 10,
						math.random(-50, 50) / 5
					)
			end
		end

		task.wait(5)
		for _,v in pairs(debrisModel:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("BasePart") then
				TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
			end
		end
		Debris:AddItem(debrisModel, 2)
	elseif type == "crucibleStrike" then
		local pos, originalPos = unpack({...})
		local a0, a1 = Storage.Miscs.lightningStrike.a0:Clone(), Storage.Miscs.lightningStrike.a1:Clone()
		a0.WorldPosition = pos
		a1.WorldPosition = originalPos or pos + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0) 
		a1.Beam.Attachment0 = a1
		a1.Beam.Attachment1 = a0
		a0.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		a1.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		local t = tick()
		while tick() - t < 0.5 do
			local curve = math.random(-150, 150) / 10
			a1.Beam.CurveSize0 = curve
			a1.Beam.CurveSize1 = curve
			task.wait(math.random(30, 80) / 1000)
		end

		a0:Destroy()
		a1:Destroy()
	end
end

Network:BindEvents({
	["client"] = function(...)
		clients(...)
	end,
})

Storage.Events.client.Event:Connect(function(...)
	clients(...)
end)

Storage.Events.killer.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
end)

local gamepasses = player:WaitForChild("gamepasses", 100)
function isUserOwnedGamepass(id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if table.find(data.id, tonumber(id)) and gamepasses:WaitForChild(name).Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function checkBlackoutWeapons()
	for _,v in pairs(workspace.Objects.Weapons:GetChildren()) do
		local highlight = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Highlight")
		if highlight and v:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") and isUserOwnedGamepass(v.gamepassId.Value) then
			highlight:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

task.wait(1)
pcall(checkBlackoutWeapons)
for _,gamepass in pairs(gamepasses:GetChildren()) do
	gamepass.Changed:Connect(checkBlackoutWeapons)
end

local function handleCharacters(character)
	pcall(function()
		task.wait(1 / 30)
		if not character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			return
		end

		task.wait(1.5)
		for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if part:GetAttribute("Enabled") ~= nil and (string.find(part.Name, "Arm") or string.find(part.Name, "Leg")) then
				part:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
					local enable = part:GetAttribute("Enabled")
					if not enable then
						createStubs(character, part)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

for _,character in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
	task.spawn(function()
		handleCharacters(character)
	end)
end
workspace.Characters.ChildAdded:Connect(handleCharacters)

-- Roblox character sound script

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
