local AtomicBinding = require(script:WaitForChild("AtomicBinding"))

local function loadFlag(flag: string)
	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return UserSettings():IsUserFeatureEnabled(flag)
	end)
	return success and result
end

local SOUND_DATA : { [string]: {[string]: any}} = {
	Climbing = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_footsteps_plastic.mp3",
		Looped = true,
	},
	Died = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/uuhhh.mp3",
	},
	FreeFalling = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_falling.mp3",
		Looped = true,
	},
	GettingUp = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_get_up.mp3",
	},
	Jumping = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_jump.mp3",
	},
	Landing = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_jump_land.mp3",
	},
	Running = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_footsteps_plastic.mp3",
		Looped = true,
		Pitch = 1.85,
	},
	Splash = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/impact_water.mp3",
	},
	Swimming = {
		SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/action_swim.mp3",
		Looped = true,
		Pitch = 1.6,
	},
}

-- map a value from one range to another
local function map(x: number, inMin: number, inMax: number, outMin: number, outMax: number): number
	return (x - inMin)*(outMax - outMin)/(inMax - inMin) + outMin
end

local function playSound(sound: Sound)
	sound.TimePosition = 0
	sound.Playing = true
end

local function shallowCopy(t)
	local out = {}
	for k, v in pairs(t) do
		out[k] = v
	end
	return out
end

local function initializeSoundSystem(instances)
	local player = instances.player
	local humanoid = instances.humanoid
	local rootPart = instances.rootPart

	local sounds: {[string]: Sound} = {}

	-- initialize sounds
	for name: string, props: {[string]: any} in pairs(SOUND_DATA) do
		local sound: Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = name

		-- set default values
		sound.Archivable = false
		sound.RollOffMinDistance = 5
		sound.RollOffMaxDistance = 150
		sound.Volume = 0.65

		for propName, propValue: any in pairs(props) do
			(sound :: any)[propName] = propValue
		end

		sound.Parent = rootPart
		sounds[name] = sound
	end

	local playingLoopedSounds: {[Sound]: boolean?} = {}

	local function stopPlayingLoopedSounds(except: Sound?)
		for sound in pairs(shallowCopy(playingLoopedSounds)) do
			if sound ~= except then
				sound.Playing = false
				playingLoopedSounds[sound] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	-- state transition callbacks.
	local stateTransitions: {[Enum.HumanoidStateType]: () -> ()} = {
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
			playSound(sounds.GettingUp)
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
			playSound(sounds.Jumping)
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming] = function()
			local verticalSpeed = math.abs(rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y)
			if verticalSpeed > 0.1 then
				sounds.Splash.Volume = math.clamp(map(verticalSpeed, 100, 350, 0.28, 1), 0, 1)
				playSound(sounds.Splash)
			end
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds(sounds.Swimming)
			sounds.Swimming.Playing = true
			playingLoopedSounds[sounds.Swimming] = true
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall] = function()
			sounds.FreeFalling.Volume = 0
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds(sounds.FreeFalling)
			playingLoopedSounds[sounds.FreeFalling] = true
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
			local verticalSpeed = math.abs(rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y)
			if verticalSpeed > 75 then
				sounds.Landing.Volume = math.clamp(map(verticalSpeed, 50, 100, 0, 1), 0, 1)
				playSound(sounds.Landing)
			end
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds(sounds.Running)
			sounds.Running.Playing = true
			playingLoopedSounds[sounds.Running] = true
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing] = function()
			local sound = sounds.Climbing
			if math.abs(rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y) > 0.1 then
				sound.Playing = true
				stopPlayingLoopedSounds(sound)
			else
				stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
			end
			playingLoopedSounds[sound] = true
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
		end,

		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead] = function()
			stopPlayingLoopedSounds()
			playSound(sounds.Died)
		end,
	}

	-- updaters for looped sounds
	local loopedSoundUpdaters: {[Sound]: (number, Sound, Vector3) -> ()} = {
		[sounds.Climbing] = function(dt: number, sound: Sound, vel: Vector3)
			sound.Playing = vel.Magnitude > 0.1
		end,

		[sounds.FreeFalling] = function(dt: number, sound: Sound, vel: Vector3): ()
			if vel.Magnitude > 75 then
				sound.Volume = math.clamp(sound.Volume + 0.9*dt, 0, 1)
			else
				sound.Volume = 0
			end
		end,

		[sounds.Running] = function(dt: number, sound: Sound, vel: Vector3)
			sound.Playing = vel.Magnitude > 0.5 and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.5
		end,
	}

	-- state substitutions to avoid duplicating entries in the state table
	local stateRemap: {[Enum.HumanoidStateType]: Enum.HumanoidStateType} = {
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics] = Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running,
	}

	local activeState: Enum.HumanoidStateType = stateRemap[humanoid:GetState()] or humanoid:GetState()

	local function transitionTo(state)
		local transitionFunc: () -> () = stateTransitions[state]

		if transitionFunc then
			transitionFunc()
		end

		activeState = state
	end

	transitionTo(activeState)

	local stateChangedConn = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, state)
		state = stateRemap[state] or state
		
		print(state)
		if state ~= activeState then
			transitionTo(state)
		end
	end)

	local steppedConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, worldDt: number)
		-- update looped sounds on stepped
		for sound in pairs(playingLoopedSounds) do
			local updater: (number, Sound, Vector3) -> () = loopedSoundUpdaters[sound]

			if updater then
				updater(worldDt, sound, rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity)
			end
		end
	end)

	local function terminate()
		stateChangedConn:Disconnect()
		steppedConn:Disconnect()

		-- Unparent all sounds and empty sounds table
		-- This is needed in order to support the case where initializeSoundSystem might be called more than once for the same player,
		-- which might happen in case player character is unparented and parented back on server and reset-children mechanism is active.
		for name: string, sound: Sound in pairs(sounds) do
			sound:Destroy()
		end
		table.clear(sounds)
	end

	return terminate
end

local binding = AtomicBinding.new({
	humanoid = "Humanoid",
	rootPart = "HumanoidRootPart",
}, initializeSoundSystem)

local playerConnections = {}

local function characterAdded(character)
	binding:bindRoot(character)
end

local function characterRemoving(character)
	binding:unbindRoot(character)
end

local function playerAdded(player: Player)
	local connections = playerConnections[player]
	if not connections then
		connections = {}
		playerConnections[player] = connections
	end

	if player.Character then
		characterAdded(player.Character)
	end
	table.insert(connections, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(characterAdded))
	table.insert(connections, player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(characterRemoving))
end

local function playerRemoving(player: Player)
	local connections = playerConnections[player]
	if connections then
		for _, conn in ipairs(connections) do
			conn:Disconnect()
		end
		playerConnections[player] = nil
	end

	if player.Character then
		characterRemoving(player.Character)
	end
end

for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
	task.spawn(playerAdded, player)
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(playerAdded)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(playerRemoving)

  03:11:16.028  PATH: StarterPlayer.StarterPlayerScripts.RbxCharacterSounds.AtomicBinding  -  Editar

  03:11:16.028  --!nonstrict
local ROOT_ALIAS = "root"

local function parsePath(pathStr)
	local pathArray = string.split(pathStr, "/")
	for idx = #pathArray, 1, -1 do
		if pathArray[idx] == "" then
			table.remove(pathArray, idx)
		end
	end
	return pathArray
end

local function isManifestResolved(resolvedManifest, manifestSizeTarget)
	local manifestSize = 0
	for _ in pairs(resolvedManifest) do
		manifestSize += 1
	end

	assert(manifestSize <= manifestSizeTarget, manifestSize)
	return manifestSize == manifestSizeTarget
end

local function unbindNodeDescend(node, resolvedManifest)
	if node.instance == nil then
		return -- Do not try to unbind nodes that are already unbound
	end

	node.instance = nil

	local connections = node.connections
	if connections then
		for _, conn in ipairs(connections) do
			conn:Disconnect()
		end
		table.clear(connections)
	end

	if resolvedManifest and node.alias then
		resolvedManifest[node.alias] = nil
	end

	local children = node.children
	if children then
		for _, childNode in pairs(children) do
			unbindNodeDescend(childNode, resolvedManifest)
		end
	end
end

local AtomicBinding = {}
AtomicBinding.__index = AtomicBinding

function AtomicBinding.new(manifest, boundFn)
	local dtorMap = {} -- { [root] -> dtor }
	local connections = {} -- { Connection, ... }
	local rootInstToRootNode = {} -- { [root] -> rootNode }
	local rootInstToManifest = {} -- { [root] -> { [alias] -> instance } }

	local parsedManifest = {} -- { [alias] = {Name, ...} }
	local manifestSizeTarget = 1 -- Add 1 because root isn't explicitly on the manifest	
	
	for alias, rawPath in pairs(manifest) do
		parsedManifest[alias] = parsePath(rawPath)
		manifestSizeTarget += 1
	end

	return setmetatable({
		_boundFn = boundFn,
		_parsedManifest = parsedManifest,
		_manifestSizeTarget = manifestSizeTarget,
		
		_dtorMap = dtorMap,
		_connections = connections,
		_rootInstToRootNode = rootInstToRootNode,
		_rootInstToManifest = rootInstToManifest,
	}, AtomicBinding)
end

function AtomicBinding:_startBoundFn(root, resolvedManifest)
	local boundFn = self._boundFn
	local dtorMap = self._dtorMap
	
	local oldDtor = dtorMap[root]
	if oldDtor then
		oldDtor()
		dtorMap[root] = nil
	end

	local dtor = boundFn(resolvedManifest)
	if dtor then
		dtorMap[root] = dtor
	end
end

function AtomicBinding:_stopBoundFn(root)
	local dtorMap = self._dtorMap
	
	local dtor = dtorMap[root]
	if dtor then
		dtor()
		dtorMap[root] = nil
	end
end

function AtomicBinding:bindRoot(root)
	debug.profilebegin("AtomicBinding:BindRoot")
	
	local parsedManifest = self._parsedManifest
	local rootInstToRootNode = self._rootInstToRootNode
	local rootInstToManifest = self._rootInstToManifest
	local manifestSizeTarget = self._manifestSizeTarget
	
	assert(rootInstToManifest[root] == nil)

	local resolvedManifest = {}
	rootInstToManifest[root] = resolvedManifest

	debug.profilebegin("BuildTree")

	local rootNode = {}
	rootNode.alias = ROOT_ALIAS
	rootNode.instance = root
	if next(parsedManifest) then
		-- No need to assign child data if there are no children
		rootNode.children = {}
		rootNode.connections = {}
	end

	rootInstToRootNode[root] = rootNode

	for alias, parsedPath in pairs(parsedManifest) do
		local parentNode = rootNode

		for idx, childName in ipairs(parsedPath) do
			local leaf = idx == #parsedPath
			local childNode = parentNode.children[childName] or {}

			if leaf then
				if childNode.alias ~= nil then
					error("Multiple aliases assigned to one instance")
				end

				childNode.alias = alias

			else
				childNode.children = childNode.children or {}
				childNode.connections = childNode.connections or {}
			end

			parentNode.children[childName] = childNode
			parentNode = childNode
		end
	end

	debug.profileend() -- BuildTree

	-- Recursively descend into the tree, resolving each node.
	-- Nodes start out as empty and instance-less; the resolving process discovers instances to map to nodes.
	local function processNode(node)
		local instance = assert(node.instance)

		local children = node.children
		local alias = node.alias
		local isLeaf = not children

		if alias then
			resolvedManifest[alias] = instance
		end

		if not isLeaf then
			local function processAddChild(childInstance)
				local childName = childInstance.Name
				local childNode = children[childName]
				if not childNode or childNode.instance ~= nil then
					return
				end

				childNode.instance = childInstance
				processNode(childNode)
			end

			local function processDeleteChild(childInstance)
				-- Instance deletion - Parent A detects that child B is being removed
				--    1. A removes B from `children`
				--    2. A traverses down from B,
				--       i.  Disconnecting inputs
				--       ii. Removing nodes from the resolved manifest
				--    3. stopBoundFn is called because we know the tree is no longer complete, or at least has to be refreshed
				-- 	  4. We search A for a replacement for B, and attempt to re-resolve using that replacement if it exists.
				-- To support the above sanely, processAddChild needs to avoid resolving nodes that are already resolved.

				local childName = childInstance.Name
				local childNode = children[childName]

				if not childNode then
					return -- There's no child node corresponding to the deleted instance, ignore
				end

				if childNode.instance ~= childInstance then
					return -- A child was removed with the same name as a node instance, ignore
				end

				self:_stopBoundFn(root) -- Happens before the tree is unbound so the manifest is still valid in the destructor.
				unbindNodeDescend(childNode, resolvedManifest) -- Unbind the tree

				assert(childNode.instance == nil) -- If this triggers, unbindNodeDescend failed

				-- Search for a replacement
				local replacementChild = instance:FindFirstChild(childName)
				if replacementChild then
					processAddChild(replacementChild)
				end
			end

			for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
				processAddChild(child)
			end

			table.insert(node.connections, instance.ChildAdded:Connect(processAddChild))
			table.insert(node.connections, instance.ChildRemoved:Connect(processDeleteChild))
		end

		if isLeaf and isManifestResolved(resolvedManifest, manifestSizeTarget) then
			self:_startBoundFn(root, resolvedManifest)
		end
	end

	debug.profilebegin("ResolveTree")
	processNode(rootNode)
	debug.profileend() -- ResolveTree
	
	debug.profileend() -- AtomicBinding:BindRoot
end

function AtomicBinding:unbindRoot(root)
	local rootInstToRootNode = self._rootInstToRootNode
	local rootInstToManifest = self._rootInstToManifest
	
	self:_stopBoundFn(root)

	local rootNode = rootInstToRootNode[root]
	if rootNode then
		local resolvedManifest = assert(rootInstToManifest[root])
		unbindNodeDescend(rootNode, resolvedManifest)
		rootInstToRootNode[root] = nil
	end

	rootInstToManifest[root] = nil
end

function AtomicBinding:destroy()
	debug.profilebegin("AtomicBinding:destroy")

	for _, dtor in pairs(self._dtorMap) do
		dtor:destroy()
	end
	table.clear(self._dtorMap)

	for _, conn in ipairs(self._connections) do
		conn:Disconnect()
	end
	table.clear(self._connections)

	local rootInstToManifest = self._rootInstToManifest
	for rootInst, rootNode in pairs(self._rootInstToRootNode) do
		local resolvedManifest = assert(rootInstToManifest[rootInst])
		unbindNodeDescend(rootNode, resolvedManifest)
	end
	table.clear(self._rootInstToManifest)
	table.clear(self._rootInstToRootNode)

	debug.profileend()
end

return AtomicBinding

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local function isHumanoid(part)
	if part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		return part.Parent
	elseif part.Parent ~= workspace then
		return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
	end

	return false
end

function getDirection(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).unit
end

function angleBetween(vectorA, vectorB)
	return math.acos(math.clamp(vectorA:Dot(vectorB), -1, 1))
end

local function isHead(part, character)
	if string.lower(part.Name) == "head" then
		return true
	else
		local weld = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld", true)
		if weld then
			if (weld.Part0 and string.lower(weld.Part0.Name) == "head") or (weld.Part1 and string.lower(weld.Part1.Name) == "head") then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function getWeldOwnerFromPart(part, character)
	local weld = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld", true)
	if weld then
		return (weld.Part0 and part:IsDescendantOf(weld.Part0) and weld.Part0) or (weld.Part1 and part:IsDescendantOf(weld.Part1) and weld.Part1)
	elseif part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
		return character.Torso
	end

	return false
end

local function isObject(part)
	return part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects)
end

function hasArmor(character, limbName)
	local armorType = ArmorDatabase.LimbsToArmorType[limbName]
	if armorType and character:FindFirstChild("Armor") and character.Armor:FindFirstChild(armorType) and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Equipped") and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Health") > 0 then
		return true
	end

	return false
end

local function createHitParticles(origin, results)
	if not _G.onScreen(results.Position) then
		return
	end
	
	local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
	attachment.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(results.Position, origin - results.Position)
	attachment.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	local materialName,_ = string.gsub(tostring(results.Instance.Material), "Enum.Material.", "")
	local materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects:FindFirstChild(materialName)
	if not materialFolder then
		materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects.Concrete
	end
	for _,p in pairs(materialFolder:GetChildren()) do
		if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			local n = p:Clone()
			--n.Color = ColorSequence.new(results.Instance.Color)
			n.Parent = attachment
			n:Emit(n:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
		end
	end
	_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Particles.HitEffectsOLD[materialFolder.Name].Sounds), attachment):Play()
	Debris:AddItem(attachment, 3)
end

local function createMarkHole(result, part, position, scale, size, ids, color, spark)

		scale = 128

	surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * Width, scale * Height)
	surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
	surfaceGui.Face = face

	local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
	surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

	local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	decal.Image = ids[math.random(#ids)]
	decal.ImageColor3 = color
	decal.Rotation = math.random(-90, 90)
	decal.ImageTransparency = 0.2
	decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
	decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	decal.Size = UDim2.new( 0, scale * size, 0, scale * size)
	decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	decal.Position = UDim2.new(RelativeX, 0, RelativeY, 0)
	decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

	surfaceGui.Parent = part
	Debris:AddItem(surfaceGui, 15)
	--[[if math.random(2) < 2 and spark and _G.onScreen(position) then

		spark.CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position + (result.Normal.unit * 0.05))
		spark.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		TweenService:Create(spark, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), CFrame = CFrame.new(result.Position)}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(spark, 1)
	end]]
end

function nearestNormalId(part, direction)
	local maxDot, maxNormal = 0, nil
	for _, normalId in ipairs(Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems()) do
		local normal = part.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.fromNormalId(normalId))
		local dot = normal:Dot(Vector3.new(math.abs(direction.x), math.abs(direction.y), math.abs(direction.z))) --Greater the more similar the vectors are
		if dot > 0 and dot > maxDot then
			maxDot = dot
			maxNormal = normalId
		end
	end

	return maxNormal
end

local function handleMeleeHitbox(settings, animTrack, humanoid, hitbox, t, attackAnim, character, targets, finisher)
	local animConnections = {}
	table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal("start"):Connect(function()
		table.clear(targets)
		if (humanoid.RootPart and _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) and getDistance(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, humanoid.RootPart) < 30) or character == player.Character then
			hitbox:HitStart()
		end
		
		local soundFolder = Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(t.ToolTip) or settings.soundSource and Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(settings.soundSource)
		if soundFolder and soundFolder.Sounds:FindFirstChild("Swing") and t:IsDescendantOf(character) and t._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.handle or "Handle") then
			_G.createSound(soundFolder.Sounds.Swing, t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"], nil, nil, 0.2):Play()
		end

		if t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
			t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = true
		end
	end))

	table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal("end"):Connect(function()
		hitbox:HitStop()
		if t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
			t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = false
		end
	end))

	table.insert(animConnections, animTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
		hitbox:HitStop()
		for _,con in pairs(animConnections) do
			con:Disconnect()
		end
		_G.clearTable(animConnections)
		animConnections = nil
		attackAnim = nil
		if character == player.Character then
			_G.meleeAttack = false
			if finisher then
				_G.meleeFinisher = false
			end
		end
		if t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
			t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = false
		end
		table.clear(targets)
	end))
end

local function manipulateDrag(Particle, Distance)
	local LargestSize = 0

	local Keypoints = Particle.Size.Keypoints
	for Index = 1, #Keypoints do
		local Size = Keypoints[Index].Value
		if Size > LargestSize then
			LargestSize = Size
		end
	end

	local Speed = Particle.Speed.Max
	local Time = math.max(Distance - LargestSize, 0) / Speed
	local N = 1.5/ Time

	Particle.Drag = N
end

local function handleAnimationSounds(settings, animTrack, markerName, t, soundName, animName, animId, connections, humanoid)
	local animConnections = {}
	local function clean()
		for _,con in pairs(animConnections) do
			con:Disconnect()
		end
		_G.clearTable(animConnections)
		animConnections = nil
		connections[animId] = nil

		if animName == "inspect" or animName == "inspect_empty" then
			pcall(function()
				for _,v in pairs(t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("Sound") and string.match(v.Name, "__sound") then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	
	table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal(markerName):Connect(function()
		if t:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and t:FindFirstChild("_mod") then
			task.defer(function()
				local soundFolder = Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(t.ToolTip) or settings.soundSource and Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(settings.soundSource)
				if soundFolder and soundFolder.Sounds:FindFirstChild(soundName) then
					local sound = _G.createSound(soundFolder.Sounds[soundName]:IsA("Folder") and getRandomChild(soundFolder.Sounds[soundName]) or soundFolder.Sounds[soundName], t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds)
					if sound then
						if t:IsDescendantOf(player.Character) then
							sound.RollOffMinDistance /= 4
							sound.Volume = sound.Volume * (_G.isFPS() and 0.8 or 1)
						end
						if soundName == "Shake" and settings.gunType and settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
							sound.Name = "_shake"
						end
						
						sound:Play()
					end
				end

				if settings.markers[animName]["type"] then
					if settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "357" then
						if markerName == "drop" then
							if _G.onScreen(t._mod.Cylinder.Position) then
								for _,bullet in pairs(t._mod.Cylinder:GetChildren()) do
									if bullet:IsA("BasePart") then
										local n = bullet:Clone()
										n.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
										n.CanCollide = true
										n.Parent = workspace.Ignored
										local soundFolder = Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(t.ToolTip) or settings.soundSource and Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(settings.soundSource)
										local sound = _G.createSound(soundFolder.Sounds[soundName], t._mod.Handle.sounds)
										if sound then
											if t:IsDescendantOf(player.Character) then
												sound.RollOffMinDistance /= 4
												sound.Volume = sound.Volume * (_G.isFPS() and 0.8 or 1)
											end
											sound:Play()
										end
										Debris:AddItem(n, 3)

										bullet.Transparency = 1
									end
								end
							end

							t._mod.Speedloader.Transparency = 0

							task.delay(animTrack.Stopped:Wait(), function()
								if t:FindFirstChild("_mod") then
									t._mod.Speedloader.Transparency = 1
									for _,bullet in pairs(t._mod.Cylinder:GetChildren()) do
										if bullet:IsA("BasePart") then
											bullet.Transparency = 0
										end
									end
								end
							end)
						elseif markerName == "load" then
							for _,bullet in pairs(t._mod.Cylinder:GetChildren()) do
								if bullet:IsA("BasePart") then
									bullet.Transparency = 0
								end
							end
						end
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "chainsaw" then
						if markerName == "rev_fx" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].SmokeStart:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v:Emit(v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
								end
							end
						end
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "crucible" then
						if markerName == "invis" then
							t._mod.Blade.Transparency = 1
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Blade:GetDescendants()) do
								if (v:IsA("Beam") or v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") or v:IsA("Trail")) and v.Name ~= "Flash" then
									v.Enabled = false
								elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
									v.Transparency = 1
								end
							end
							
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade.Idle, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Volume = 0}):Play()
						elseif markerName == "trail" then
							t._mod.Handle.Trail.Enabled = true
						elseif markerName == "spark" then
							local blade = t._mod.Blade:Clone()
							blade:ClearAllChildren()
							blade.TextureID = ""
							blade.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
							blade.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
							blade.Transparency = 0.5
							blade.Parent = t._mod.Blade
							
							local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
							weld.Part0 = t._mod.Blade
							weld.Part1 = blade
							weld.Parent = blade
							Debris:AddItem(blade, 1.5)
							TweenService:Create(blade, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(blade.Size.x + 0.1, blade.Size.y + 0.1, blade.Size.z + 0.1)}):Play()
							
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Blade.Flash:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v:Emit(v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
								end
							end
							
							if humanoid.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head") then
								for _,v in pairs(Storage.Miscs.Weapons["The Crucible"].EyesEffects:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("Attachment") then
										local n = v:Clone()
										n.Parent = humanoid.Parent.Head
										for _,k in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
											if k:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and k.Name == "Spark" then
												k:Emit(k:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
											end
										end
										task.delay(5, function()
											for _,k in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
												if k:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
													k.Enabled = false
												end
											end
										end)
										Debris:AddItem(n, 7)
									end
									
								end
								
							end
							
						elseif markerName == "visible" or markerName == "cancel" then
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 0.02}):Play()
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Blade:GetDescendants()) do
								if (v:IsA("Beam") or v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") or v:IsA("Trail")) and v.Parent.Name ~= "Flash" then
									v.Enabled = true
								elseif v.Parent.Name == "Flash" and v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v:Emit(v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
								elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
									TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
								end
							end
							
							local blade = t._mod.Blade:Clone()
							blade:ClearAllChildren()
							blade.TextureID = ""
							blade.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
							blade.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
							blade.Transparency = 0.5
							blade.Parent = t._mod.Blade
							
							local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
							weld.Part0 = t._mod.Blade
							weld.Part1 = blade
							weld.Parent = blade
							Debris:AddItem(blade, 1.5)
							TweenService:Create(blade, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(blade.Size.x + 0.1, blade.Size.y + 0.1, blade.Size.z + 0.1)}):Play()
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade.Idle, TweenInfo.new(1.5), {Volume = 0.5}):Play()
						elseif markerName == "invisible" then
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Blade:GetDescendants()) do
								if (v:IsA("Beam") or v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") or v:IsA("Trail")) and v.Name ~= "Flash" then
									v.Enabled = false
								elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then
									v.Transparency = 1
								end
							end
							
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade.Idle, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Volume = 0}):Play()
						elseif markerName == "smash" then
							local pos = t._mod.Center.Position
							local params = RaycastParams.new()
							params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, workspace.Characters}
							for k = 1, 10 do
								local origin = pos + (k ~= 1 and ((pos - humanoid.RootPart.Position).unit * (k / 2) * 1.5) or Vector3.new())
								local cast = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -10, 0), params)
								if cast then
									local a = _G.emitParticle("GroundHitEffect", nil, cast.Position, 5)
									if a then
										a.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position, cast.Position + cast.Normal)
									end
									local materialName,_ = string.gsub(tostring(cast.Instance.Material), "Enum.Material.", "")
									local materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects:FindFirstChild(materialName)
									if not materialFolder then
										materialFolder = Storage.Particles.HitEffects.Concrete
									end
									_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Particles.HitEffectsOLD[materialFolder.Name].Sounds), a or cast.Position):Play()
									local size = math.random(20, 45) / 10
									_G.createGuiSurface(
										"rbxassetid://1169338338",
										cast.Instance,
										cast.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-100, 100) / 100, 0, math.random(-100, 100) / 100),
										128,
										Vector2.new(size, size),
										{
											["ImageColor3"] = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70),
											["ImageTransparency"] = math.random(100, 250) / 1000,
											["Rotation"] = math.random(-90, 90),
										}
									)
									
									task.defer(function()
										local debrisModel = Storage.Models.Debris.ExplosionMin:Clone()
										debrisModel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(cast.Position))
										debrisModel.Parent = workspace.Ignored
										for _,v in pairs(debrisModel:GetChildren()) do
											if v:IsA("BasePart") then
												v.Color = (cast.Material == Enum.Material.Grass or cast.Material == Enum.Material.LeafyGrass or cast.Material == Enum.Material.Mud) and Color3.fromRGB(129, 115, 116) or cast.Instance.Color
												v.Material = (cast.Material == Enum.Material.Grass or cast.Material == Enum.Material.LeafyGrass or cast.Material == Enum.Material.Mud) and Enum.Material.Concrete or cast.Material
												v.Transparency = cast.Instance.Transparency
												local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
												force.Force = Vector3.new(0, v:GetMass() * 120, 0)
												force.Parent = v
												v.Anchored = false
												v.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, math.random(10, 20), 0)
													+ Vector3.new(
														math.random(-50, 50) / 5,
														math.random(50, 150) / 10,
														math.random(-50, 50) / 5
													)
											end
										end

										task.wait(5)
										for _,v in pairs(debrisModel:GetChildren()) do
											if v:IsA("BasePart") then
												TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
											end
										end
										Debris:AddItem(debrisModel, 2)
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "diy_ft" then
						if markerName == "light" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Spark:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v:Emit(v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
								end
							end

							task.wait(1 / 15)
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = true
								end
							end
						elseif markerName == "light_back" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = true
								end
							end
						elseif markerName == "close" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = false
								end
							end
						elseif markerName == "fire_lit" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Can.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = true
								end
							end
							task.delay(1 / 10, function()
								for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Can.FX:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
										v.Enabled = false
									end
								end
							end)
						elseif markerName == "start" then
							local casted = false
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Can.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									if not v.Enabled and not casted then
										casted = true
										v.Enabled = true
										
										local params = RaycastParams.new()
										params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, workspace.Characters}
										local connection
										connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
											if not v:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not v.Enabled then
												connection:Disconnect()
												return
											end
											
											local fireParticle = t._mod.Can.FX.Fire
											local cast = workspace:Raycast(t._mod.Can.FX.WorldCFrame.p, t._mod.Can.FX.WorldCFrame.UpVector * -fireParticle:GetAttribute("MaxDist"), params)
											if cast then
												manipulateDrag(fireParticle, getDistance(t._mod.Can.FX.WorldCFrame.p, cast.Position))
											else
												fireParticle.Drag = fireParticle:GetAttribute("DefaultDrag")
											end
										end)
									end
									
									v.Enabled = true
								end
							end
							
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Can.sounds.Fire, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Volume = 0.12}):Play()
						elseif markerName == "cancel" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Can.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = false
								end
							end
							
							TweenService:Create(t._mod.Can.sounds.Fire, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Volume = 0}):Play()
							local shakeSound = t._mod.Can.sounds:FindFirstChild("_shake")
							if shakeSound then
								shakeSound:Destroy()
							end
						end
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "molotov" then
						if markerName == "ignite" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Lighter.Spark:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v:Emit(v:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
								end
							end
							
							task.wait(1 / 15)
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = true
								end
							end
						elseif markerName == "light" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Cloth.Bone:GetDescendants()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v.Enabled = true
								elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
									v.Playing = true
								end
							end
							
							t._mod.Cloth.PointLight.Enabled = true
						elseif markerName == "close" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = false
								end
							end
						elseif markerName == "cancel" then
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Cloth.Bone:GetDescendants()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v.Enabled = false
								elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
									v.Playing = false
								end
							end
							t._mod.Cloth.PointLight.Enabled = false
							
							for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
									v.Enabled = false
								end
							end
						end
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "insert_shell" then
						clean()
					elseif settings.markers[animName]["type"] == "magDrop" then
						if markerName == "magDrop" and t._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.markers[animName]["magName"]) and _G.onScreen and (getDistance(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, t._mod[settings.markers[animName]["magName"]]) < 100 or _G.onScreen(t._mod[settings.markers[animName]["magName"]].Position)) then
							local fakeMag = t._mod[settings.markers[animName]["magName"]]:Clone()
							if fakeMag:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") then
								for _,v in pairs(fakeMag:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("BasePart") then
										v.CanCollide = true
										v.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
										task.delay(4, function()
											TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
										end)
									elseif v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("Motor6D") then
										v:Destroy()
									end
								end
							else
								for _,v in pairs(fakeMag:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("Motor6D") then
										v:Destroy()
									end
								end
								
								fakeMag.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
								fakeMag.CanCollide = true
							end
							
							if fakeMag.Transparency < 1 then
								task.delay(4, function()
									TweenService:Create(fakeMag, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
								end)
							end
							
							fakeMag.CanTouch = true
							fakeMag.Parent = workspace.Ignored
							if t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("magEjectDirection") then
								fakeMag.Velocity = t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].magEjectDirection.WorldCFrame.LookVector * math.random(settings.magEjectForce[1], settings.magEjectForce[2])
								fakeMag.RotVelocity = fakeMag.Velocity * 2
							end
							
							local connection
							connection = fakeMag.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
								if hit:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings) then
									connection:Disconnect()
									_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.mag_impact), fakeMag):Play()
								end
							end)
							
							Debris:AddItem(fakeMag, 5)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end))

	table.insert(animConnections, animTrack.Stopped:Connect(clean))
end

local function weld(X, Y)
	local CJ = CFrame.new(X.Position)
	local C0 = X.CFrame:inverse()*CJ
	local C1 = Y.CFrame:inverse()*CJ

	local W = Instance.new("Weld")
	W.Name = "Weld"
	W.Parent = X
	W.Part0 = X
	W.Part1 = Y
	W.C0 = C0
	W.C1 = C1
end

local hitmarkTween
local function handleCharactersActivities(character)
	pcall(function()
		--print("now monitoring " .. character.Name .. " ragdolls.")
		task.wait(1 / 30)
		if not character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			return
		end
		
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")
		local characterIK = R6IK.New(character)
		
		local ikParams = RaycastParams.new()
		ikParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character, workspace.Ignored}
		
		local onBackTool
		local lastGunTool

		local function createToolHolster()
			if lastGunTool and not lastGunTool:GetAttribute("Dropped") then
				local toolMotor = lastGunTool:FindFirstChild("Holster", true)
				if toolMotor and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
					local model = Instance.new("Model")
					model.Name = lastGunTool.Name
					for _,v in pairs(lastGunTool:GetDescendants()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "Shell" and not v:GetAttribute("IgnoreHolster") then
							local n = v:Clone()
							if v == lastGunTool.PrimaryPart then
								n.Name = "Handle"
							end
							n.Massless = true
							n.Parent = model
							--n.Name = v.Name == "Handle" and "X" or v.Name
						end
					end
					if lastGunTool.Name == "Brassknuckles" then
						for _,v in pairs(model.Handle:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("BasePart") and v:FindFirstChild("HandleWeld") then
								v.WeldWithPartName:Destroy()
								v.HandleWeld.Enabled = true
							end
						end
					end
					for _,m in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
						if (m:IsA("Motor6D") or m:IsA("Weld") or m:IsA("WeldConstraint") or m:IsA("Beam") or m:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or m:IsA("Sound") or m:IsA("Attachment") or m:IsA("Light")) and m.Name ~= "Holster" and m.Name ~= "HandleWeld" then
							m:Destroy()
						end
					end

					if lastGunTool.Name ~= "Brassknuckles" then
						for _,m in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
							if m:IsA("BasePart") then
								if m.Name == "Handle" then
									model.PrimaryPart = m
								end

								weld(model.Handle, m)
							end
						end
					end

					local motor = model:FindFirstChild("Holster", true)
					motor.Part0 = character.Torso
					motor.Part1 = motor.Parent
					motor.Name = lastGunTool.Name
					motor.Parent = character.Holsters.Motors

					local object = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
					object.Value = lastGunTool
					object.Name = "object"
					object.Parent = model

					model.Parent = character.Holsters.Models
					Storage.Events.replay:Fire("register", model)
				end

				onBackTool = lastGunTool
			end
		end		
		
		local anim_connections = {}
		local connections = {}
		local connections2 = {}
		local function tool(t)
			if not Storage.Modules.Weapons:FindFirstChild(t.Name) then
				return
			end
			
			local backTool = onBackTool and character.Holsters.Models:FindFirstChild(onBackTool.Name)
			if backTool then
				character.Holsters.Motors[onBackTool.Name]:Destroy();
				backTool:Destroy()
			end

			if t ~= lastGunTool then	
				createToolHolster()
			end
			lastGunTool = t
			
			--if character ~= player.Character then
			local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[t.Name])
			local targets = {}
			local hitbox, attackAnim
			if t:GetAttribute("Melee") or settings.anims.shove then
				hitbox = CastMelee.new(t)
				hitbox.DetectionMode = settings.chainsaw and 3 or 2
				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, character, workspace.CurrentCamera}
				params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
				hitbox.RaycastParams = params

				hitbox.OnHit:Connect(function(hit, _, result, _, point)		
					hitmarkTween = require(Storage.Modules.Library.HitboxMeleeHandler)(settings, targets, hit, humanoid, attackAnim, player, result, point, character, hitmarkTween, t)
				end)
			end
			local toolConnections = {}
			local function cleanup()
				for _,con in pairs(toolConnections) do
					con:Disconnect()
				end
				_G.clearTable(toolConnections)
				toolConnections = nil

				if t:IsDescendantOf(game) then
					pcall(function()
						for _,v in pairs(t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Sound") and string.match(v.Name, "__sound") then
								v:Destroy()
							end
						end
					end)
					
					if settings.type == "molotov" then
						for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Cloth.Bone:GetDescendants()) do
							if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
								v.Enabled = false
							elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
								v.Playing = false
							end
						end
						t._mod.Cloth.PointLight.Enabled = false

						for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
								v.Enabled = false
							end
						end
					elseif settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
						for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Can.FX:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
								v.Enabled = false
							end
						end
						
						for _,v in pairs(t._mod.Lighter.FX:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
								v.Enabled = false
							end
						end
						
						TweenService:Create(t._mod.Can.sounds.Fire, TweenInfo.new(16e-5), {Volume = 0}):Play()
					elseif settings.crucible then
						TweenService:Create(t._mod.Blade.Idle, TweenInfo.new(1 / 30), {Volume = 0}):Play()
					end
				end
			end

			table.insert(toolConnections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animTrack)
				if not animTrack then
					return
				end
				
				if hitbox then
					hitbox.Visualizer = ReplicatedStorage.__config:WaitForChild("debug").Value
				end

				if settings.ejectSniper and humanoid.RootPart then
					local dist = getDistance(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, humanoid.RootPart)
					if animTrack.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://" .. settings.anims[settings.ejectSniperData.animName] and dist < 75 and not anim_connections[settings.anims[settings.ejectSniperData.animName] .. "1"] then
						anim_connections[settings.anims[settings.ejectSniperData.animName] .. "1"] = true
						local animConnections = {}
						table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal(settings.ejectSniperData.afterMarker):Connect(function()
							if t:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
								local handle = t._mod:FindFirstChild("Handle")
								if handle and handle:FindFirstChild("shellPos") and (_G.onScreen(handle.Position) or character == player.Character) then
									task.defer(function()
										local bullet = Storage.Models.Cartridges[settings.cartridge]:Clone()
										bullet.Parent = workspace.Ignored
										bullet.Bullet.Transparency = 1
										bullet.CFrame = handle.shellPos.WorldCFrame * (settings.ejectCFrame or CFrame.new())
										bullet.Velocity = handle.shellPos.WorldCFrame.LookVector 
											* math.random(settings.ejectForce.x[1], settings.ejectForce.x[2]) 
											+ Vector3.new(0, math.random(settings.ejectForce.y[1], settings.ejectForce.y[2]), 0)
										bullet.RotVelocity = bullet.Velocity * 2
										bullet.smoke.Enabled = true

										local connection
										connection = bullet.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
											if not hit:IsDescendantOf(character) and not hit:IsDescendantOf(workspace.CurrentCamera) then
												connection:Disconnect()
												_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.casings), bullet):Play()

												task.wait(math.random(5, 20) / 100)
												if bullet:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
													bullet.smoke.Enabled = false
												end
											end
										end)
										Debris:AddItem(bullet, 3)
										Storage.Events.replay:Fire("register", bullet)
									end)
								end
							end
						end))

						table.insert(animConnections, animTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
							for _,con in pairs(animConnections) do
								con:Disconnect()
							end
							_G.clearTable(animConnections)
							animConnections = nil
							anim_connections[settings.anims[settings.ejectSniperData.animName] .. "1"] = nil
						end))
					end
				end

				for animName,animId in pairs(settings.anims) do
					if typeof(settings.anims[animName]) ~= "table" and animTrack.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://" .. animId then
						if not anim_connections[animId] then
							if settings.markers[animName] then
								anim_connections[animId] = true
								for markerName,soundName in pairs(settings.markers[animName]) do
									handleAnimationSounds(settings, animTrack, markerName, t, soundName, animName, animId, anim_connections, humanoid)
								end
							end

							if animName == "idle" and settings.chainsaw then
								local idleSFX = t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds.Idle
								idleSFX.Volume = 0
								idleSFX:Play()
								TweenService:Create(idleSFX, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Volume = idleSFX:GetAttribute("DefaultVolume")}):Play()
								for _,v in pairs(t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Smoke:GetChildren()) do
									v.Enabled = true
								end
								local connection
								connection = animTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
									connection:Disconnect()
									idleSFX:Stop()
									TweenService:Create(idleSFX, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Volume = 0}):Play()
									for _,v in pairs(t._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Smoke:GetChildren()) do
										v.Enabled = false
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					elseif typeof(settings.anims[animName]) == "table" then
						for _,animId in pairs(settings.anims[animName]) do
							if animTrack.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://" .. animId and settings.markers[tostring(animId)] then
								if not anim_connections[animId] then
									anim_connections[animId] = true
									for markerName,soundName in pairs(settings.markers[tostring(animId)]) do
										handleAnimationSounds(settings, animTrack, markerName, t, soundName, tostring(animId), animId, anim_connections, humanoid)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if (typeof(settings.anims[animName]) == "table" and animName == "attack" or animName == "finisher" or animName == "shove") and t:IsDescendantOf(character) then
						local kill = false
						if (animName == "finisher" or animName == "shove") and animTrack.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://" .. animId then
							attackAnim = animTrack
							handleMeleeHitbox(settings, animTrack, humanoid, hitbox, t, attackAnim, character, targets, true)
						elseif typeof(settings.anims[animName]) == "table" and animName == "attack" then
							for animName2,animId2 in pairs(settings.anims[animName]) do
								if animTrack.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://" .. animId2 then
									attackAnim = animTrack
									handleMeleeHitbox(settings, animTrack, humanoid, hitbox, t, attackAnim, character, targets, false)

									kill = true
									break
								end
							end
						end

						if kill then
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end))
			
			if t:WaitForChild("_at"):FindFirstChild("SniperScope") and character ~= player.Character then
				table.insert(toolConnections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					local part = t._at.SniperScope
					if _G.onScreen(part.Position) then
						local lookForward = part.CFrame.LookVector
						local lookToPoint = (workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position - part.Position).Unit
						local angle = angleBetween(lookForward, lookToPoint)

						TweenService:Create(part.Glare.UI.Icon.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Scale = math.abs(angle) <= math.rad(5) and getDistance(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, part) > 50 and humanoid:GetAttribute("Aim") and 1 or 0}):Play()
					end
				end))
			end

			table.insert(toolConnections, t.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
				if parent ~= character and parent ~= nil then
					cleanup()
					
					task.wait(1 / 30)
					if not character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") and #character.Holsters.Models:GetChildren() < 1 and not t:GetAttribute("Dropped") then
						lastGunTool = t
						local backTool = onBackTool and character.Holsters.Models:FindFirstChild(onBackTool.Name)
						if backTool then
							character.Holsters.Motors[onBackTool.Name]:Destroy();
							backTool:Destroy()
						end

						createToolHolster()
					end
				end
			end))
			
			--[[task.wait(1 / 30)
			Replay:Register(t)]]
			--end
		end

		humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdoll"):Connect(function()
			local state = humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
			if humanoid.RootPart and state and getDistance(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, humanoid.RootPart.Position) < 150 then
				for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if part:IsA("BasePart") then
						local lastVel = Vector3.new()
						local lastImpactSFX
						table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							local vel = part.Velocity
							if lastVel.magnitude - vel.magnitude > 0.25 and (not lastImpactSFX or not lastImpactSFX:IsDescendantOf(game)) then
								--[[local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
								a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
								a.WorldPosition = part.Position
								a.Visible = true
								Debris:AddItem(a, 3)]]
								local impact = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.ragdoll_impacts), part, nil, 0.65)
								if impact then
									impact.PlaybackSpeed = impact.PlaybackSpeed + (math.random() * -0.2)
									impact:Play()
									lastImpactSFX = impact
								end
							end

							lastVel = vel
						end))
					end
				end
			else
				for _,con in pairs(connections) do
					con:Disconnect()
				end
				_G.clearTable(connections)
			end
		end)
		
		humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Downed"):Connect(function()
			local state = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
			if state then
				while character:IsDescendantOf(game) and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") do
					local blood_settings = {
						["spreadness"] = 2,
						["decal_amount"] = math.random(1, 2),
						["process"] = {5, 20, 100},
						["size"] = {60, 500, 100},
						["mode"] = "track",
						["parent"] = humanoid.RootPart,
						["drain"] = true,
					}
					
					Storage.Events.client:Fire("handleBlood", character, humanoid.RootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -20, 0), {character, workspace.Ignored}, blood_settings)
					
					task.wait(math.random(40, 60) / 100)
				end
			end
		end)
		
		local connection
		connection = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_,state)
			if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
				connection:Disconnect()
				
				if character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
					for _,sound in pairs(character.Head:GetChildren()) do
						if sound.Name == "hurt" then
							sound:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

				--[[if character ~= Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
					local deadMark = Storage.Miscs.deadMark:Clone()
					deadMark.Parent = character

					deadMark.OutlineTransparency = 1

				end]]
			end
		end)

		table.insert(connections2, character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
			if parent == nil then
				table.clear(characterIK)
				characterIK = nil
				for _,con in pairs(connections) do
					con:Disconnect()
				end
				_G.clearTable(connections)

				for _,con in pairs(connections2) do
					con:Disconnect()
				end
				_G.clearTable(connections2)

					if typeof(con) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
						con:Disconnect()
					end
				end
				_G.clearTable(anim_connections)
			end
		end))

		if replicatePlayer or character:GetAttribute("AI") then
			character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(fromTool)
				if fromTool:IsA("Tool") and fromTool:GetAttribute("Arms") then
					tool(fromTool)
				end
			end)
			for _,fromTool in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if fromTool:IsA("Tool") and fromTool:GetAttribute("Arms") then
					tool(fromTool)
				end
			end

			local rightHipJoint = character.Torso:WaitForChild("Right Hip")
			local leftHipC0 = leftHipJoint.C0
			local rightHipC0 = rightHipJoint.C0
			local rendered = false

			local lastLeftHitPos, lastRightHitPos
			table.insert(connections2, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
				local newDt = dt * 60

				if humanoid.RootPart then			
					if _G.onScreen and _G.onScreen(humanoid.RootPart.Position) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Carried") then
						rendered = true

						local force = humanoid.RootPart.Velocity * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
						local leftFootStart, leftFootEnd, leftFootEnd2 = humanoid.RootPart:FindFirstChild("LeftFootStart"), character:FindFirstChild("LeftFootAttachment", true), character:FindFirstChild("LeftFootEnd", true)
						local rightFootStart, rightFootEnd, rightFootEnd2 = humanoid.RootPart:FindFirstChild("RightFootStart"), character:FindFirstChild("RightFootAttachment", true), character:FindFirstChild("RightFootEnd", true)
						local leftIKC0, rightIKC0 = false, false
						if leftFootStart and leftFootEnd and rightFootStart and rightFootEnd and leftFootEnd2 and rightFootEnd2 
							and	(replicatePlayer == player and not _G.isCrouch and not _G.isProne or replicatePlayer ~= player and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Crouch") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Prone"))
							and force.magnitude <= 3 and humanoid.HipHeight >= -0.1 and not humanoid.Sit and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then

							local leftFootCast = workspace:Raycast(leftFootStart.WorldCFrame.p, leftFootEnd2.WorldCFrame.p - leftFootStart.WorldCFrame.p, ikParams)
							local topSurface = leftFootCast and nearestNormalId(leftFootCast.Instance, leftFootCast.Normal)
							if leftFootCast and (not topSurface or topSurface == Enum.NormalId.Top) and leftFootCast.Instance.CanCollide and leftFootCast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
								leftIKC0 = characterIK:LegIK("Left", leftFootCast.Position)
							end

							local rightFootCast = workspace:Raycast(rightFootStart.WorldCFrame.p, rightFootEnd2.WorldCFrame.p - rightFootStart.WorldCFrame.p, ikParams)
							local topSurface = rightFootCast and nearestNormalId(rightFootCast.Instance, rightFootCast.Normal)
							if rightFootCast and (not topSurface or topSurface == Enum.NormalId.Top) and rightFootCast.Instance.CanCollide and rightFootCast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
								rightIKC0 = characterIK:LegIK("Right", rightFootCast.Position)
							end
						end

						local tiltZ, direction = 0, Vector3.new()
						if force.magnitude > 2 then
							direction = force.unit
							tiltZ = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.RightVector:Dot(direction)
						end

						local rootTiltZ, leftHipTiltY, rightHipTiltY = 0, 0, 0
						if humanoid.MoveDirection:Dot(humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.LookVector) > -0.1 then
							--rootTiltZ = math.rad(-tiltZ * 30)
							leftHipTiltY = math.rad(-tiltZ * 40)
							rightHipTiltY = math.rad(-tiltZ * 40)
						else
							--rootTiltZ = math.rad(tiltZ * 30)
							leftHipTiltY = math.rad(tiltZ * 40)
							rightHipTiltY = math.rad(tiltZ * 40)
						end

						local lerpAlpha = force.magnitude > 3 and 0.08 or 0.35
						leftHipJoint.C0 = leftHipJoint.C0:Lerp((leftIKC0 or leftHipC0) * CFrame.Angles(0, leftHipTiltY, 0), lerpAlpha * newDt)
						rightHipJoint.C0 = rightHipJoint.C0:Lerp((rightIKC0 or rightHipC0) * CFrame.Angles(0, leftHipTiltY, 0), lerpAlpha * newDt)
					elseif rendered then
						rendered = false
						leftHipJoint.C0 = leftHipJoint.C0
						rightHipJoint.C0 = rightHipJoint.C0
					end
				end
			end))
		end
	end)
end

--Replay.VPF.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE
Storage.Events.replay.Event:Connect(function(type, ...)
	if true then
		return
	end
	
	if type == "register" then
		Replay:Register(...)
	elseif type == "str" then
		Replay:StopRecording(...)
	elseif type == "sr" then
		Replay:StartRecording(...)
	elseif type == "s" then
		Replay:Stop(...)
		Replay.VPF.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE
	elseif type == "p" then
		Replay:Play(...)
		Replay.VPF.Parent = workspace.Ignored
	elseif type == "clr" then
		Replay:ClearRecording()
	end
end)

Storage.Events.replayFunc.OnInvoke = function(type, metaData)
	if true then
		return false
	end
	
	if type == "folder" then
		return Replay.VPF
	elseif type == "meta" and Replay[metaData] then
		return Replay[metaData]
	end

	return false
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
	if not process then
		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadTwo then
			print("stop record")
			Replay:StopRecording()
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadOne then
			print("start record")
			Replay:StartRecording()
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadFour then
			print("stop playing")
			Replay:Stop(1)
			Replay.VPF.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadThree then
			print("start playing")
			Replay:Play(1)
			Replay.VPF.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadFive then
			print("clear recording")
			Replay:ClearRecording()
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadSix then
			print("asking last record")
			Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "record", Replay.FrameTimes)
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadSeven then
			print(Replay.FrameTimes)
		end
	end
end)

_G.clientDebugStats = {
	renderFps = 0,
	physicsFps = 0,
	
	totalMbUsage = 0,
	graphicsUsage = 0,
	luaUsage = 0,
	physicsUsage = 0,
	dataSend = 0,
	dataRecieve = 0,
	packetLoss = 0,
	lagging = false,
	
	screenSize = {
		x = 0,
		y = 0
	},
	
	clientLatency = 0,
	
	threads = 0,

local TimeFunction = RunService:IsRunning() and time or os.clock

local LastIteration, Start
local FrameUpdateTable = {}

local packetRecieved = false
local packetSent = 0
local packetLoss = 0
local lastPacketLoss = 0
local packetLossCount = 0
local packet_timeout = 1 / 5
local function sendPacket()
	local start = tick()
	Network:InvokeServer("pingResponse")
	packetRecieved = true
	return tick() - start
end

local function HeartbeatUpdate()
	if ReplicatedStorage.__config:FindFirstChild("stats") and ReplicatedStorage.__config.stats.Value == true then
		LastIteration = TimeFunction()
		for Index = #FrameUpdateTable, 1, -1 do
			FrameUpdateTable[Index + 1] = FrameUpdateTable[Index] >= LastIteration - 1 and FrameUpdateTable[Index] or nil
		end

		FrameUpdateTable[1] = LastIteration
		_G.clientDebugStats.renderFps = tostring(math.floor(TimeFunction() - Start >= 1 and #FrameUpdateTable or #FrameUpdateTable / (TimeFunction() - Start)))
		_G.clientDebugStats.physicsFps = workspace:GetRealPhysicsFPS()
		
		_G.clientDebugStats.totalMbUsage = StatsService:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb()
		local graphicsUsageMb = StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsParts) 
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsTerrain) 
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsTexture)
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsMeshParts)
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsParticles)
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsSolidModels)
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsSpatialHash)
			+ StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.GraphicsTextureCharacter)
		_G.clientDebugStats.graphicsUsage = graphicsUsageMb
		_G.clientDebugStats.luaUsage = StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.LuaHeap)
		_G.clientDebugStats.physicsUsage = StatsService:GetMemoryUsageMbForTag(Enum.DeveloperMemoryTag.PhysicsParts)
		_G.clientDebugStats.dataSend = StatsService.DataSendKbps
		_G.clientDebugStats.dataRecieve = StatsService.DataReceiveKbps
		
		local viewportSize = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
		_G.clientDebugStats.screenSize.x = viewportSize.x
		_G.clientDebugStats.screenSize.y = viewportSize.y
	end
end

Start = TimeFunction()
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(HeartbeatUpdate)

local function setCore()
	local setted,_ = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, false)
	end)

	if not setted then
		return setCore()
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		packetSent += 1
		task.spawn(function()
			local ms = sendPacket()
			_G.clientDebugStats.clientLatency = ms
			--print(ms .. " ms")
		end)
		local timer = tick()
		while tick() - timer < packet_timeout do
			if packetRecieved then
				break
			end
			
			task.wait()
		end
		
		if not packetRecieved then
			packetLoss += 1
			packetLossCount += 1
		else
			packetLossCount = 0
		end
		
		local packetLossPercentage = (packetLoss / packetSent) * 100
		if packetLossPercentage < 1 then
			packetLoss = 0
			packetSent = 0
			packetLossPercentage = 0
		end
		_G.clientDebugStats.lagging = packetLossPercentage > lastPacketLoss or packetLossCount > 5
		_G.clientDebugStats.packetLoss = packetLossPercentage
		packetRecieved = false
		lastPacketLoss = packetLossPercentage
		
		task.wait()
	end
end)

local defaultWalkSpeed = 17
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

local function isOccluded(v3)
	local p = RaycastParams.new()
	p.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
	p.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
	--[[local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
	a.WorldPosition = camera.CFrame.Position + (v3 - camera.CFrame.Position).unit * ((getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, v3) * 0.85))
	a.Visible = true
	a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
	Debris:AddItem(a, 3)
	if c then
		local b = Instance.new("Part")
		b.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.Position)
		b.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
		b.Transparency = 0.5
		b.Anchored = true
		b.CanCollide = false
		b.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		Debris:AddItem(b, 3)
	end]]

	return workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, (v3 - camera.CFrame.Position).unit * ((getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, v3) * 0.95)), p)
end

local function isInDoor(v3)
	local p = RaycastParams.new()
	p.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
	p.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

	local inDoor = false
	local c = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), p)
	if c and c.Instance.Size.magnitude > 10 then
		return c
	end

	return inDoor
end

function tableContains(tbl, val)
	for i = 1, #tbl do
		if val == tbl[i] then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function removeValue(tbl, val)
	for i = 1, #tbl do
		if val == tbl[i] then
			table.remove(tbl, i)
			return
		end
	end
end

function playSound(s, parent, pitch, vol, self)
	local sound = typeof(s) == "Instance" and s:Clone() or Instance.new("Sound")
	if typeof(s) ~= "Instance" then
		sound.SoundId = s
	end
	sound.RollOffMinDistance = 0.5 * (not self and 13.5 or 6.5)
	sound.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.Inverse
	sound.RollOffMaxDistance = 500
	sound.Volume = vol or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound.Name = "__sound"
	sound.Parent = parent
	sound:Play()
	Debris:AddItem(sound, 1)
	
	if sound.Parent:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and (sound.Parent:IsA("BasePart") or sound.Parent:IsA("Attachment")) then
		sound.SoundGroup = game:GetService("SoundService").Game
		local emitPosition = sound.Parent:IsA("Attachment") and sound.Parent.WorldPosition or sound.Parent.Position
		local result = isOccluded(emitPosition)
		if result then
			local equalizer = Storage.Miscs.Equalizer:Clone()
			equalizer.LowGain = getDistance(camera.CFrame.Position, result.Position)
			equalizer.MidGain = -equalizer.LowGain
			equalizer.Parent = sound
		--[[else
			local inDoorResult = isInDoor(emitPosition)
			if inDoorResult then
				local reverb = Storage.Miscs.Reverb:Clone()
				reverb.WetLevel = math.min(-55 + getDistance(inDoorResult.Position, emitPosition), -8)
				reverb.Parent = sound
			end]]
		end
	end
	
	return sound
end

function listenToHumanoid(player, humanoid)
	if tableContains(listeningTo, humanoid) then
		error("desynchronization detected. something attempted to register more than one loop on the same humanoid object.")
	end
	
	listeningTo[#listeningTo + 1] = humanoid
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local connection
	local latest = tick()
	local update = tick()
	local climbing = false
	local swimming = false
	local lastMaterial = Enum.Material.Concrete
	local footstepsEmitPoint = Instance.new("Attachment")
	footstepsEmitPoint.Position = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
	footstepsEmitPoint.Name = "FootstepsEmitPoint"
	footstepsEmitPoint.Parent = humanoid.Parent.Torso
	
	local generate = function(play, type)
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {humanoid.Parent, workspace.CurrentCamera, workspace.Ignored}
		local ray = workspace:Raycast(humanoid.RootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, play and -5 or -3.5, 0), params)
		if (ray or climbing or swimming) and (ray and ray.Instance.Velocity.magnitude < 1 or tick() - update > 0.35 or play) then
			update = tick()
			local material = climbing and Enum.Material.ForceField or swimming and Enum.Material.Water or ray.Material
			local soundList = meta:GetSoundsForMaterial(type == "jump" and lastMaterial or material, type)
			if soundList and #soundList:GetChildren() > 0 then
				local sound = soundList:GetChildren()[math.random(#soundList:GetChildren())]
				playSound(sound, footstepsEmitPoint, climbing and 1.25 + math.random() * 0.15 or nil, sound.Volume, player == Players.LocalPlayer)
				
				lastMaterial = material
			end
		end
	end
	
	local swim_sound = script.swimming:Clone()
	swim_sound.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
	
	humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(prev, current)
		if current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed and humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -10 then
			if humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -35 then
				--[[local land = script.land:Clone()
				land.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
				land:Play()
				Debris:AddItem(land, 1)]]
				
				generate(true, "land")
			end
		elseif current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
			--[[local jump = script.jump:Clone()
				--jump.TimePosition = 0.22
				jump.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
				jump:Play()
				Debris:AddItem(jump, 1)]]
			generate(true, "jump")
		elseif current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -10 then
			local splash = script.splash:Clone()
			splash.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
			splash:Play()
			Debris:AddItem(splash, splash.TimeLength + 1)
			
			_G.emitParticle("SplashEffect", nil, humanoid.RootPart.Position)
		end
		
		climbing = current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing
		swimming = current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming
		
		if player == Players.LocalPlayer then
			local s,_ = string.gsub(tostring(current), "Enum.HumanoidStateType.", "")
			Storage.Events.humState:FireServer(s)
		end
	end)
	
	if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
		humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StateCurrent"):Connect(function()
			local current = humanoid:GetAttribute("StateCurrent")
			local prev = humanoid:GetAttribute("StatePrevious")
			
			if current == "Landed" and humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -10 then
				if humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -35 then
					--[[local land = script.land:Clone()
					land.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
					land:Play()
					Debris:AddItem(land, 1)]]

					generate(true, "land")
				end
			elseif current == "Jumping" then
				--[[local jump = script.jump:Clone()
				--jump.TimePosition = 0.22
				jump.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
				jump:Play()
				Debris:AddItem(jump, 1)]]
				generate(true, "jump")
			elseif current == "Swimming" and humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y < -10 then
				local splash = script.splash:Clone()
				splash.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
				splash:Play()
				Debris:AddItem(splash, splash.TimeLength + 1)

				_G.emitParticle("SplashEffect", nil, humanoid.RootPart.Position)
			end

			climbing = current == "Climbing"
			swimming = current == "Swimming"
		end)
	end
	
	connection = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not humanoid or not humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not humanoid.RootPart or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			--warn("desynchronized sound-stepping from character instance " .. player.Name)
			swim_sound:Stop()
			removeValue(listeningTo, humanoid)
			connection:Disconnect()
			return
		end
		
		swim_sound.Playing = swimming
		
		local distanceFromCamera = camera.CFrame.Position - humanoid.RootPart.Position
		local calculateDistance = math.min(distanceFromCamera.magnitude / 5000, 0.35)
		local linearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.x, 0, humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.z)
		if climbing then
			linearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.y * 2, 0)
		end
		local wt = (1 / 3) * defaultWalkSpeed / (linearVelocity.magnitude + 0.5)
		if calculateDistance < 0.1 then
			calculateDistance = 0
		end
		if tick() - latest > math.max(wt + calculateDistance, 0.15) and distanceFromCamera.magnitude < 500 then
			latest = tick()
		else
			return
		end
		
		if humanoid.MoveDirection ~= Vector3.new() or not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(humanoid.Parent) then
			generate(nil, linearVelocity.magnitude > 15 and "run")
		end
	end)
end

function prepareListener(player)
	local setup = function(player)
		local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		listenToHumanoid(player, humanoid)
	end
	
	task.wait(1)
	setup(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(1) setup(player) end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(prepareListener)
for _,player in pairs(Players:GetChildren()) do
	prepareListener(player)
end

task.wait(1)
for _,character in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
	if character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character) and character:GetAttribute("ActiveAI") then
		listenToHumanoid(false, character.Humanoid)
	end
end
workspace.Characters.ChildAdded:Connect(function(character)
	task.wait(1)
	if character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character) and character:GetAttribute("ActiveAI") then
		listenToHumanoid(false, character.Humanoid)
	end
end)  -  Editar

local soundsByMaterial = {}

	for i,sound in pairs(data.Sounds) do
		if type(sound) == "number" then
			data.Sounds[i] = "rbxassetid://" .. (tostring(sound))
		end
	end
	
	for _, material in pairs(data.Materials) do
		soundsByMaterial[material] = data.Sounds
	end
end

local preloadedSounds = {}
for material, sounds in pairs(soundsByMaterial) do
	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
	folder.Name = "token" .. tostring(game:GetService("HttpService"):GenerateGUID(false))
	folder.Parent = script
	preloadedSounds[material] = folder
	
	for _,soundId in pairs(sounds) do
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "meta" .. tostring(game:GetService("HttpService"):GenerateGUID(false))
		sound.SoundId = soundId
		--sound.SoundGroup = game.SoundService.General
		sound.Parent = folder
	end
end

function meta:GetSoundsForMaterial(material)
	return preloadedSounds[material]
end

function meta:GetRawLookup()
	return audioLookup
end

  03:11:16.031  --[[
	PlayerModule - This module requires and instantiates the camera and control modules,
	and provides getters for developers to access methods on these singletons without
	having to modify Roblox-supplied scripts.

	2018 PlayerScripts Update - AllYourBlox
--]]

local PlayerModule = {}
PlayerModule.__index = PlayerModule

function PlayerModule.new()
	local self = setmetatable({},PlayerModule)
	self.cameras = require(script:WaitForChild("CameraModule"))
	self.controls = require(script:WaitForChild("ControlModule"))
	return self
end

function PlayerModule:GetCameras()
	return self.cameras
end

function PlayerModule:GetControls()
	return self.controls
end

function PlayerModule:GetClickToMoveController()

end

return PlayerModule.new()
