local function worldToScreen(viewPoint)
	local ToObjectSpace = camera.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(viewPoint)
	local FieldOfView = math.tan(math.rad(camera.FieldOfView) / 2)
	return Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X * (0.5 + 0.5 * (ToObjectSpace.x / ToObjectSpace.z / -(camera.ViewportSize.X / camera.ViewportSize.Y * FieldOfView))), camera.ViewportSize.Y * (0.5 + 0.5 * (ToObjectSpace.y / ToObjectSpace.z / FieldOfView)))
end

UI.Cursor.Visible = true
table.insert(connections, character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(tool)
	if tool:IsA("Tool") and (isAimable() or tool:GetAttribute("Melee")) then
		toolVM = true
		if not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") and not tool:GetAttribute("Grenade") then
			UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.gunInfo.Visible = true
		end
		local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[tool.Name])
		Crosshair:Set(settings.crosshairRadius or 20, true, nil, nil, 0.25)
		local handle = tool._mod:WaitForChild(settings.handle or "Handle")
		local connections = {}

		local rootJoint = humanoid.RootPart:WaitForChild("RootJoint")
		local rootJointC0 = rootJoint.C0

		local rightShoulder = character.Torso:WaitForChild("Right Shoulder")
		local leftShoulder = character.Torso:WaitForChild("Left Shoulder")

		local function cleanup()
			Storage.Events.client:Fire("crosshair", true, 0)
			Crosshair:Set(0, true, nil, nil, 0.25)

				connections[i] = nil
			end
			connections = nil

			if rightShoulder then
				TweenService:Create(rightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {C0 = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 1.55, 0)}):Play()
			end
			if leftShoulder then
				TweenService:Create(leftShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {C0 = CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -1.55, 0)}):Play()
			end

			_G.aim = false
			isActuallyAiming = false
			if humanoid.Health > 0 then
				UI.Cursor.Center.Visible = true
				for _,v in pairs(UI.Cursor:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("Frame") and v.Name ~= "Center" then
						v.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if settings.noCrosshair then
			UI.Cursor.Center.Visible = false
		end

		for _,v in pairs(UI.Cursor:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Frame") and v.Name ~= "Center" then
				v.Visible = true
			end
		end

		local lasers = {}
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, character, camera}
		params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist

		if not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") then
			for _,obj in pairs(tool._at:GetDescendants())do
				if obj:IsA("BasePart") and string.match(obj.Name, "ProjectorSight") then
					local scope = obj.Parent
					local guis = {}
					for _,v in pairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
							guis[#guis + 1] = v
						end
					end

					local parts = {}
					for _,v in pairs(tool._mod:GetDescendants()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Transparency < 1 then
							parts[#parts + 1] = {p = v, t = v.Transparency}
						end
					end

					if scope.Name == "SniperScope" then
						for _,v in pairs(scope:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "ProjectorSight" and v.Name ~= "AimPart" then
								TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
							end
						end

						TweenService:Create(scope.AimPart, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
						TweenService:Create(scope, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
					end

					local aim = false
					table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:connect(function()
						--local size = camera.FieldOfView / 70
						--reticle.Size = UDim2.new(0, sizeX * size, 0, sizeY * size)

						local dist = getDistance(tool._mod.Handle.aimPos.WorldPosition, camera.CFrame.Position)
						if scope.Name == "SniperScope" then
							if dist < 0.35 and not aim then
								aim = true
								for _,v in pairs(scope:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "ProjectorSight" and v.Name ~= "AimPart" then
										TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
									end
								end

								TweenService:Create(scope.AimPart, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
								TweenService:Create(scope, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
							elseif dist >= 0.35 and aim then
								aim = false
								for _,v in pairs(scope:GetChildren()) do
									if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "ProjectorSight" and v.Name ~= "AimPart" then
										TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
									end
								end

								TweenService:Create(scope.AimPart, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
								TweenService:Create(scope, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
							end

							--[[for _,v in pairs(parts) do
								v.p.Transparency = _G.aim and 1 or v.t
							end]]
						end

						for _,gui in pairs(guis) do
							local reticle = gui.ClippingFrame.Reticle
							local sizeX, sizeY = gui.ClippingFrame.Reticle.Size.X.Offset, gui.ClippingFrame.Reticle.Size.Y.Offset

							if dist < 0.35 then
								gui.Enabled = true
								local dist_scale = obj.CFrame:pointToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame.Position) / obj.Size
								reticle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 + dist_scale.x, 0, 0.5 - dist_scale.y, 0)
							else
								gui.Enabled = false
							end
						end
					end))

					if settings.sniper then
						UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.steadyBar.bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
						local swayness = 0
						local breathe = 800
						local maxBreathe = 800
						table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:connect(function(dt)
							local newDt = dt * 60

							if _G.aim and _G.isFPS() then
								local x = math.sin(tick() * 10 * 0.35) * swayness
								local y = math.cos(tick() * 20 * 0.35) * swayness
								local bob = Vector3.new(x, y, 0)
								camera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(math.rad(bob.y * newDt), math.rad(bob.x  * newDt), 0)
							end

							if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and breathe > 0 and _G.aim then
								swayness = lerp(swayness, 0, 0.025 * newDt)
								breathe = math.min(breathe - 0.5 * newDt, maxBreathe)
							else
								swayness = lerp(swayness, 0.025, 0.08 * newDt)
								breathe = math.min(breathe + 0.45 * newDt, maxBreathe)
							end

							UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.steadyBar.bar.Size = UDim2.new((breathe / maxBreathe) * 1, 0, 1, 0)
							UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.steadyBar.Visible = aim
						end))
					end
				elseif obj:IsA("Model") and obj.Name == "Laser" then
					lasers[#lasers + 1] = obj
				end
			end
		end

		local wallParams = RaycastParams.new()
		wallParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
		wallParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

		local oldCamCF
		local camTiltZ = 0
		local zOffset, zOffsetTarget = 0, 0
		local aimPos = not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") and tool._mod.Handle:FindFirstChild("aimPos")
		local muzzlePos = not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") and tool._mod.Handle:FindFirstChild("MuzzleFX")
		local bobSize, swaySize, swayMultiplier = 0.75, 1, 1

			x = 0,

		}

		table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
			if not humanoid.RootPart or humanoid.Health < 1 then
				cleanup()
				return
			end

			local newDt = dt * 60

			local motor = tool:FindFirstChild("Motor")
			if not _G.isFPS() then
				if motor then
					motor.Part0 = settings.leftArmMotor and character["Left Arm"] or character["Right Arm"]
				elseif tool._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.handle or "Handle") then
					if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("NoMotorWeld") then
						tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].NoMotorWeld.Part0 = character[tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].NoMotorWeld:GetAttribute("TargetPart")]
					end

					for _,part in pairs(tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							local weld = part:FindFirstChild("WeldWithPartName")
							if weld and character:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
								weld.Part0 = character[part.Name]
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif _G.isFPS() and vm then
				if motor then
					motor.Part0 = settings.leftArmMotor and vm["Left Arm"] or vm["Right Arm"]
				elseif tool._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.handle or "Handle") then
					if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("NoMotorWeld") then
						tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].NoMotorWeld.Part0 = vm[tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].NoMotorWeld:GetAttribute("TargetPart")]
					end

					for _,part in pairs(tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							local weld = part:FindFirstChild("WeldWithPartName")
							if weld and vm:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
								weld.Part0 = vm[part.Name]
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if aimPos and not _G.gunEquip then
					local dist = getDistance(tool._mod.Handle.aimPos.WorldPosition, camera.CFrame.Position)
					local lerpTime = settings.aimLerp or 0.15
					if dist < 0.008 and _G.aim then
						isActuallyAiming = true
					elseif not _G.aim then
						isActuallyAiming = false
					end

					TweenService:Create(UI.vignette, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {ImageTransparency = _G.aim and 0 or 0.45}):Play()
					aimCF = _G.aim and aimCF:lerp(aimPos.WorldCFrame:toObjectSpace(vm.Head.CFrame), (isActuallyAiming and settings.aimLock and 0.5 or lerpTime) * newDt) or aimCF:lerp(CFrame.new(_G.isCrouch and not _G.reload and not settings.noTacCrouch and not _G.inspect and -0.6 or 0, _G.isCrouch and not _G.reload and not settings.noTacCrouch and not _G.inspect and -0.35 or 0, -0.5) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(_G.isCrouch and not _G.reload and not settings.noTacCrouch and not _G.inspect and 30 or 0)), 0.1 * newDt)
				else
					aimCF = CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.5)
				end

				if muzzlePos then
					local cast = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, muzzlePos.WorldCFrame.Position - camera.CFrame.Position, wallParams)
					if cast and not _G.aim then
						zOffsetTarget = getDistance(muzzlePos.WorldCFrame.Position, cast.Position)
					else
						zOffsetTarget = 0
					end
				end
			end

			if not _G.isFPS() and humanoid.Health > 0 --[[and (not _G.meleeFinisherAnim and settings.chainsaw or not settings.chainsaw)]] then
				if rightShoulder then
					TweenService:Create(rightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(armOffset + -math.asin((mouse.Origin.p - mouse.Hit.p).unit.y), 1.55, 0)}):Play()
				end
				if leftShoulder then
					TweenService:Create(leftShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(armOffset + -math.asin((mouse.Origin.p - mouse.Hit.p).unit.y), -1.55, 0)}):Play()
				end
			else
				if rightShoulder then
					TweenService:Create(rightShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = CFrame.new(1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 1.55, 0)}):Play()
				end
				if leftShoulder then
					TweenService:Create(leftShoulder, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {C0 = CFrame.new(-1, 0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, -1.55, 0)}):Play()
				end
			end

			if neck then
				neckAngle = lerp(neckAngle, _G.aim and -30 or 0, 0.08 * newDt)
			end

			if not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") then
				for _,obj in pairs(lasers) do
					local part = obj.LaserPoint
					local toPoint = part.To
					local beam = part.Beam
					beam.Enabled = true
					for _,p in pairs(part:GetDescendants()) do
						if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
							p.Enabled = true
						end
					end

					local partToCast = part.CFrame
					if not _G.isFPS() then
						partToCast = part.CFrame
					end
					local cast = workspace:Raycast(partToCast.Position, partToCast.LookVector * part.Range.Value, params)
					if cast then
						toPoint.Hit.Enabled = true
						toPoint.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position + (cast.Normal.unit * 0.05))
					else
						toPoint.Hit.Enabled = false
						toPoint.WorldPosition = part.Position + partToCast.LookVector * part.Range.Value
					end
				end
			end

			if vm then
				if dt < 0.9 then
					bobSize = _G.aim and lerp(bobSize, 0.25, 0.25 * newDt) or lerp(bobSize, 0.75, 0.1 * newDt)
					swaySize = _G.aim and lerp(swaySize, 0.15, 0.25 * newDt) or lerp(swaySize, 2, 0.1 * newDt)
					swayMultiplier = _G.aim and lerp(swayMultiplier, 0.05, 0.25 * newDt) or lerp(swayMultiplier, 1, 0.1 * newDt)
					--local bob = Vector3.new(bobbing(10), bobbing(5), bobbing(5))
					local vel = math.min(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.magnitude, 20)
					local bob = Vector3.new(0.5 * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 14) * math.sin(tick() * 8), 0.5 * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 14) * math.cos(tick() * 16), 0)
					local bobANG = Vector3.new(math.rad(1 * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 6) * math.sin(tick() * 16)), math.rad(1 * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 6) * math.cos(tick() * 8)), 0)
					SP_bobbing:shove(((bob / 10) * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 10)) * bobSize * newDt)
					SP_bobbingAngle:shove(((bobANG / 2.5) * (math.random(vel / 1.35, vel) / 10)) * bobSize * newDt)
					local updatebob = SP_bobbing:update(dt)
					local updatebobANG = SP_bobbingAngle:update(dt)
					local updateAim = SP_aim:update(dt)
					local updateRecoil = SP_recoil:update(dt)
					local updateRecoilANG = SP_recoilANG:update(dt)
					walkCF = walkCF:Lerp(CFrame.new(updatebob.x, updatebob.y, 0) * CFrame.Angles(updatebobANG.x, updatebobANG.y, updatebobANG.z), 0.1 * newDt)
					aimSpringCF = CFrame.new(updateAim.x, updateAim.y, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, updateAim.z) * CFrame.new(updateRecoil.x, updateRecoil.y, updateRecoil.z) * CFrame.Angles(updateRecoilANG.x, updateRecoilANG.y, updateRecoilANG.z)

					--local rotation = camera.CFrame:toObjectSpace(lastCameraCF)
					--local x,y,z = rotation:ToOrientation()
					--local angX, angY = math.clamp(math.sin(x) * swaySize, -0.1, 0.1), math.clamp(math.sin(y) * swaySize, -0.25, 0.25)
					camTiltZ = camera.CFrame:vectorToObjectSpace(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity / 10).x
					local mouseDelta = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
					local deltaX, deltaY = (mouseDelta.x / 400) * swayMultiplier, (mouseDelta.y / 400) * swayMultiplier
					SP_sway:shove(Vector3.new(math.clamp(deltaY, -0.15 * newDt, 0.15 * newDt), math.clamp(deltaX, -0.15 * newDt, 0.15 * newDt), -math.rad(clamp(camTiltZ, -25, 25))))
					--SP_sway:shove(Vector3.new(angX * swayMultiplier, angY * swayMultiplier, 0))
					local updateSway = SP_sway:update(dt)
					swayCF = CFrame.Angles(updateSway.x, updateSway.y, updateSway.y / 1.5) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, updateSway.z)
					--swayCF = swayCF:Lerp(CFrame.Angles(angX, angY, 0), 0.15 * newDt)
				end

				for _,motor in pairs(vm:GetDescendants()) do
					local realMotor = character:FindFirstChild(motor.Name, true)
					if motor:IsA("Motor6D") and realMotor and realMotor:IsA("Motor6D") and ((not string.find(string.lower(motor.Name), "root") and not string.find(string.lower(motor.Name), "neck")) or tool:GetAttribute("Melee") or stamina.state) then
						if not motor:GetAttribute("default") then
							motor:SetAttribute("default", motor.Transform)
						end

						motor.Transform = realMotor.Transform
					elseif motor:GetAttribute("default") then
						motor.Transform = motor.Transform:Lerp(motor:GetAttribute("default"), 0.1 * newDt)
					end
				end

				local linearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y, 0, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.z)
				if (_G.run and settings.leftArmDisabledOn.sprint) or (_G.aim and settings.leftArmDisabledOn.aim) or (_G.reload and settings.leftArmDisabledOn.reload) then
					vm["Left Arm"].Transparency = 1
				else
					vm["Left Arm"].Transparency = 0
				end

				for _,part in pairs(vm:GetChildren()) do
					if part:IsA("BasePart") and character:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
						part.Color = character[part.Name].Color
					end
				end

				if fakeCam:IsDescendantOf(character) and humanoid.RootPart and _G.isFPS() then
					local newCamCF = fakeCam.CFrame:toObjectSpace(humanoid.RootPart.CFrame)
					if oldCamCF then
						local _, _, z = newCamCF:toOrientation()
						local x, y, _ = newCamCF:toObjectSpace(oldCamCF):toEulerAnglesXYZ()
						fakeCamCF.x = lerp(fakeCamCF.x, x, 0.5 * newDt)
						fakeCamCF.y = lerp(fakeCamCF.y, y, 0.5 * newDt)
						fakeCamCF.z = lerp(fakeCamCF.z, z, 0.5 * newDt)
						camera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(fakeCamCF.x, fakeCamCF.y, -fakeCamCF.z)

						fakeCamAngles = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesXYZ(fakeCam.Cam.Transform:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()) or CFrame.new()
					end

					oldCamCF = newCamCF
				end

				local v = worldToScreen((camera.CFrame * fakeCamAngles:Inverse() * CFrame.new(0, 0, -4)).Position)
				local v2 = camera.ViewportSize / 2
				UI.Cursor.Position = _G.isFPS() and UDim2.new(0, v.x, 0, v.y) or UDim2.new(0, mouse.x, 0, mouse.y + 36)
				vm.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = camera.CFrame * swayCF * aimCF * walkCF * CFrame.new(0, -1.5, zOffset) * aimSpringCF * fakeCamAngles:Inverse()
				zOffset = lerp(zOffset, zOffsetTarget, 0.1 * newDt)
			end

			lastCameraCF = camera.CFrame
		end))

		table.insert(connections, tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
			if parent ~= character then
				cleanup()
			end
		end))
	elseif tool:IsA("Accoutrement") and ReplicatedStorage.__config.accessories.Value == false then
		task.wait(1 / 30)
		if tool:FindFirstChild("BodyBackAttachment", true) then
			tool.Handle.Transparency = 1
		end
	end
end))

local function changeCameraMode(mode)
	if mode == "TPS" then
		TweenService:Create(cameraOffset, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Value = Vector3.new(1.75, 0, 0)}):Play()
	elseif mode == "FPS" then
		TweenService:Create(humanoid, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential), {CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)}):Play()
	end

	cameraMode = mode
end
changeCameraMode(cameraMode)

function promptMenu(type, data)
	if UI:FindFirstChild("__prompt") then
		UI.__prompt:Destroy()
	end
	--UI.HUD.GroupTransparency = 1
	HUD(false)
	HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local connections = {}
	local returnData
	local recieved
	local prompt = type == 1 and Storage.Miscs.prompt:Clone() or type == 2 and Storage.Miscs.promptPerms:Clone()
	prompt.header.Text = data.header
	prompt.header.TextColor3 = data.header_color or prompt.header.TextColor3
	local size = string.len(data.desc)
	prompt.desc_frame.desc.Size = UDim2.new(0, prompt.desc_frame.desc.Size.X.Offset, 0, size)
	prompt.desc_frame.desc.Text = data.desc
	prompt.promptButton1.button.Text = data.button1 or "CLOSE"
	if type == 2 then
		prompt.promptButton2.button.Text = data.button2 or "OK"
	end
	prompt.Name = "__prompt"
	prompt.Parent = UI
	prompt.ZIndex = 1e5
	prompt.UIScale.Scale = 0.8
	TweenService:Create(prompt.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Scale = 1}):Play()
	--playerGui.SOUNDS.ui_show:Play()
	for _,button in pairs(prompt:GetDescendants()) do
		if button:IsA("TextButton") or button:IsA("ImageButton") then
			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				recieved = button.Parent.Name == "promptButton1" and 1 or 2
				event:Fire()
			end)
			handleButtonEvents(button)
		end
	end

	prompt.Destroying:Connect(function()
		event:Fire()
	end)

	event.Event:Wait()
	for _,con in pairs(connections) do
		con:Disconnect()
	end
	_G.clearTable(connections)
	pcall(function() prompt:Destroy() end)
	--UI.HUD.GroupTransparency = 0
	HUD(true)
	if canDisplayGameplayHUD() then
		HUD_GAMEPLAY(true)
	end

	return recieved, data
end

local generalStorePage = "foods"
local function updateGeneralStorePage()
	for _,v in pairs(UI.HUD.cv_store.cv_category:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Frame") then
			v.UIStroke.Color = v.Name == generalStorePage and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 7)
			if v.Name == generalStorePage then
				v.UIScale.Scale = 0.6
				TweenService:Create(v.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic), {Scale = 1}):Play()
			end
		end
	end

	for _,v in pairs(UI.HUD.cv_store:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Frame") and string.find(string.lower(v.Name), "_page") then
			v.Visible = v.Name == generalStorePage .. "_PAGE"
		end
	end
end
updateGeneralStorePage()

function replayFunction(button)
	if true then return end
	if UI.deathFrame.panel.Visible and not button:GetAttribute("_pressed") and not respawning and killerPlayer ~= false and killerPlayer ~= nil then
		button:SetAttribute("_pressed", true)
		local folder = Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("folder")
		local targetCharacter, selfCharacter
		local function getNewCharacters()
			for _,v in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == killerPlayer.Name and v:FindFirstChild("Head") and not v:GetAttribute("NonActive") then
					targetCharacter = v
					break
				end
			end

			for _,v in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == player.Name and v:FindFirstChild("Head") and not v:GetAttribute("NonActive") then
					selfCharacter = v
					break
				end
			end
		end
		if folder then
			getNewCharacters()
		end

		if targetCharacter and selfCharacter then
			TweenService:Create(UI.fade, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(SoundService.Game, TweenInfo.new(1), {Volume = 0}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(SoundService.Replay, TweenInfo.new(1), {Volume = 1}):Play()
			local noReplay = Instance.new("StringValue")
			noReplay.Name = "noReplay"
			noReplay.Parent = button
			replaying = true
			task.wait(1)
			UI.replaySkip.Visible = true
			local postFXs = {}
			for _,v in pairs(camera:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") then
					if v.Enabled then
						v.Enabled = false
						postFXs[#postFXs + 1] = v
					end
				end
			end
			UI.deathFrame.Visible = false
			UI.hurtOverlay.Visible = false
			for _,v in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
				if v ~= character then
					v.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.characters
				end
			end
			local parts = {}
			for _,v in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") then
					v.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.clean
					parts[#parts + 1] = v
				end
			end
			local function updateCamCF()
				if not targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head") or not selfCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head") then
					getNewCharacters()
				end
				local headCF = targetCharacter.Head.CFrame
				local selfHeadCF = selfCharacter.Head.CFrame
				local dir = (selfHeadCF.Position - headCF.Position).unit * -10
				return CFrame.new(headCF.Position + dir, selfHeadCF.Position) * CFrame.new(5, 0, 0)
			end			
			_G.deathTween = TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = updateCamCF()})
			_G.deathTween:Play()
			local recordingTime = Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("meta", "RecordingTime")
			local timeStamp = 0
			local replaySecond = 5
			if recordingTime > replaySecond and recordingTime - replaySecond > replaySecond then
				timeStamp = recordingTime - replaySecond
			end
			Storage.Events.replay:Fire("p", nil, timeStamp)
			getNewCharacters()
			local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
			highlight.FillTransparency = 1
			highlight.Parent = selfCharacter
			task.delay(0.5, function()
				TweenService:Create(UI.fade, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
			end)
			local clean, clean1, clean2 = false, false, false
			local cameraCF = camera.CFrame
			local inputConnection
			inputConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
				if not process and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
					clean = true
					inputConnection:Disconnect()
					inputConnection = nil
				end
			end)
			local connection
			connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				_G.deathTween = TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {CFrame = updateCamCF()})
				_G.deathTween:Play()

				if not Storage.Events.replayFunc:Invoke("meta", "Playing") and not clean1 then
					clean1 = true
					task.wait(1)
					clean = true
				end

				if clean and not clean2 then
					connection:Disconnect()
					UI.replaySkip.Visible = false
					if inputConnection then
						inputConnection:Disconnect()
					end
					clean2 = true
					TweenService:Create(SoundService.Game, TweenInfo.new(1), {Volume = 1}):Play()
					TweenService:Create(SoundService.Replay, TweenInfo.new(1), {Volume = 0}):Play()

					TweenService:Create(UI.fade, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
					task.wait(0.75)
					Storage.Events.replay:Fire("s")
					Storage.Events.replay:Fire("clr")
					highlight:Destroy()
					UI.deathFrame.Visible = true
					UI.hurtOverlay.Visible = true
					for index,data in pairs(postFXs) do
						data.Enabled = true
						postFXs[index] = nil
					end
					postFXs = nil
					for _,v in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.characters:GetChildren()) do
						v.Parent = workspace.Characters
					end
					for index,part in pairs(parts) do
						part.Parent = character
						parts[index] = nil
					end
					parts = nil
					replaying = false
					task.wait(1.5)
					TweenService:Create(UI.fade, TweenInfo.new(1), {Transparency = 1}):Play()
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end

function respawnFunction(button)
	if deathRespawn and UI.deathFrame.panel.Visible and not button:GetAttribute("_pressed") and not respawning and not replaying then
		respawning = true
		button:SetAttribute("_pressed", true)
		button.Text = "RESPAWNING"
		TweenService:Create(UI.deathFrame.panel, TweenInfo.new(1.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1.05, 0)}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(UI.fade, TweenInfo.new(1.5), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
		task.wait(1.5)
		Network:FireServer("vars", "respawn")
	end
end

local currentZIndex = 2
function buttonListener(button, name)
	if not buttonsActive then
		return false
	end

	if name == "cvClose" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD()
		playerGui.SOUNDS.ui_close:Play()
		if canDisplayGameplayHUD() then
			HUD_GAMEPLAY(true)
		end
	elseif name == "cvPurchase" then
		local storeInfo = UI.HUD.cv_store.itemInfo
		local itemData = GeneralStoreItems[storeInfo.category.Value][storeInfo.item.Value]
		local prompt = promptMenu(2, {
			header = "PURCHASE CONFIRMATION",
			header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 127),
			desc = "Are you sure you want to buy " .. storeInfo.item.Value .. " for £" .. itemData.price .. "?",
			button1 = "CANCEL",
			button2 = "CONFIRM"
		})

		if prompt == 2 then
			local purchaseState, messageData = Network:InvokeServer("reqBuy", storeInfo.category.Value, storeInfo.item.Value, lastSeller, "cv_st")
			if purchaseState then
				playerGui.SOUNDS.ui_buy:Play()
			else
				playerGui.SOUNDS.error:Play()
			end

			promptMenu(1, messageData)
		end
	elseif button:IsDescendantOf(UI.HUD.cv_store.cv_category) then
		generalStorePage = button.Parent.Name
		playerGui.SOUNDS.ui_show:Play()
		updateGeneralStorePage()
	elseif name == "settingsButton" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD(UI.HUD.settingsFrame)
		UI.HUD.settingsFrame.Visible = true
		HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
	elseif name == "settingsClose" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD()
		if canDisplayGameplayHUD() then
			HUD_GAMEPLAY(true)
		end
	elseif name == "loadoutButton" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD(UI.HUD.loadoutFrame)
		UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.Visible = true
		HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
	elseif name == "loadoutConfirm" then
		if ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == false or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") then
			Network:FireServer("vars", "loadout", {})
		else
			promptMenu(1, {
				header = "UNABLE TO CHANGE LOADOUT",
				header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
				desc = "Changing your loadout is only possible with PVP disabled or when you have a force field.",
				button1 = "CLOSE",
			})
		end

		closeOtherWindowsHUD()
		if canDisplayGameplayHUD() then
			HUD_GAMEPLAY(true)
		end
	elseif name == "shopButton" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD(UI.HUD.shopFrame)
		UI.HUD.shopFrame.Visible = true
		HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
	elseif name == "shopClose" then
		closeOtherWindowsHUD()
		if canDisplayGameplayHUD() then
			HUD_GAMEPLAY(true)
		end
	elseif string.find(name, "SETTINGS_") then
		settings(button, name)
	elseif name == "respawnButton" then
		respawnFunction(button)
	elseif name == "replayButton" then
		replayFunction(button)
	elseif button:IsDescendantOf(UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout) then
		updateLoadoutLists(name)
	end
end

function handleButtonEvents(button)
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		playerGui.SOUNDS.hover:Play()
	end)

	button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local clickSound = _G.createSound(playerGui.SOUNDS.click, playerGui)
		if clickSound then
			clickSound.PlaybackSpeed += math.random() * 0.4
			clickSound:Play()
		end

		local clickEffect = Storage.Miscs.clickEffect:Clone()
		clickEffect.Position = UDim2.new(0, mouse.x, 0, mouse.y + 36)
		clickEffect.ZIndex = 10000
		clickEffect.Parent = UI
		TweenService:Create(clickEffect, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 70), ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(clickEffect, 1)

		buttonListener(button, button.Name)
	end)
end

for _,button in pairs(UI:GetDescendants()) do
	if button:IsA("TextButton") or button:IsA("ImageButton") then
		handleButtonEvents(button)
	end
end

local itemGeneralStoreLists = {}
for catagory,item in pairs(GeneralStoreItems) do
	for itemName,itemData in pairs(item) do
		local storeInfo = UI.HUD.cv_store.itemInfo
		local frame = Storage.Miscs.shopItemListFrame:Clone()
		frame.itemName.Text = string.upper(itemName)
		frame.itemDesc.Text = string.upper(itemData.desc)
		frame.itemIcon.Image = itemData.image

		handleButtonEvents(frame.shopItemInfoButton.shopItemInfoButton)
		frame.shopItemInfoButton.shopItemInfoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			for _,v in pairs(storeInfo.stats:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Frame") then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for _,v in pairs(itemGeneralStoreLists) do
				v.UIStroke.Color = v == frame and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB()
			end

			storeInfo.icon.image.Image = itemData.image
			storeInfo.itemName.Text = itemName
			storeInfo.price.Text = "£" .. toComma(itemData.price)
			storeInfo.category.Value = catagory
			storeInfo.item.Value = itemName

			for statName,statValue in pairs(itemData.stats) do
				local statFrame = Storage.Miscs.statsInfoFrame:Clone()
				statFrame.type.Text = statName
				statFrame.value.Text = statValue
				statFrame.Parent = storeInfo.stats
			end
		end)

		frame.Parent = UI.HUD.cv_store[catagory .. "_PAGE"].list
		itemGeneralStoreLists[#itemGeneralStoreLists + 1] = frame
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	for devProdName,data in pairs(Products) do
		local canBulkBuy = data.bulkBuyProdId > 0
		local frame = Storage.Miscs[canBulkBuy and "productOption2" or "productOption1"]:Clone()
		local productInfo, bulkProductInfo = MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(data.prodId, Enum.InfoType.Product), canBulkBuy and MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(data.bulkBuyProdId, Enum.InfoType.Product)
		frame.itemName.Text = devProdName
		frame.itemImage.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. productInfo.IconImageAssetId
		frame.itemPrice1.Text = utf8.char(0xE002) .. " " .. productInfo.PriceInRobux
		frame.Parent = UI.HUD.shopFrame.main.devProductLists
		handleButtonEvents(frame.buyOne)
		frame.buyOne.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			MarketplaceService:PromptProductPurchase(player, data.prodId)
		end)

		if canBulkBuy then
			frame.bulkBuy.Text = "BUY X" .. data.bulkBuyAmount
			frame.itemPrice2.Text = utf8.char(0xE002) .. " " .. bulkProductInfo.PriceInRobux
			frame.itemPrice2_discount.Text = [[<s>]] .. utf8.char(0xE002) .. " " .. productInfo.PriceInRobux * 6 .. [[</s>]]

			handleButtonEvents(frame.bulkBuy)
			frame.bulkBuy.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				MarketplaceService:PromptProductPurchase(player, data.bulkBuyProdId)
			end)
		else
			frame.itemPrice2_discount.Text = [[<s>]] .. utf8.char(0xE002) .. " " .. productInfo.PriceInRobux * 2 .. [[</s>]]
		end
	end

	for gamepassName,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		local frame = Storage.Miscs.productOption1:Clone()
		local productInfo = MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(data.id[#data.id], Enum.InfoType.GamePass)
		frame.itemName.Text = productInfo.Name
		frame.itemImage.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. productInfo.IconImageAssetId
		frame.itemPrice1.Text = utf8.char(0xE002) .. " " .. productInfo.PriceInRobux
		frame.itemPrice2_discount.Text = ""
		frame.buyOne.Text = "BUY"
		frame.Parent = UI.HUD.shopFrame.main.gamepassLists
		handleButtonEvents(frame.buyOne)
		frame.buyOne.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, data.id[#data.id])
		end)
	end
end)

function settings_action(button, data, state)
	if data.var == "accessories" then
		for _, item in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if item:IsA("Accessory") and item:FindFirstChild("BodyBackAttachment", true) then
				item.Handle.Transparency = state and 0 or 1
			end
		end
	elseif data.var == "pvp" and button.canSet.Value == true then
		if not humanoid:FindFirstChild("inPvpMode") then
			local text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
			if button.init.Value == true then
				Network:FireServer("vars", "pvp", {mode = state})
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				if not state and button.init.Value == true then
					button.canSet.Value = false

					for k = 10, 1, -1 do
						button.Text = string.format("(%s)", k)
						task.wait(1)
					end
				end

				button.Text = text
				button.init.Value = true
				button.canSet.Value = true
			end)
		else
			promptMenu(1, {
				header = "Unable to change",
				header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
				desc = [[You cannot configure this setting while in a PVP arena.]],
				button1 = "CLOSE",
			})

			ReplicatedStorage.__config[data.var].Value = true
			button.Text = "ON"
		end
	elseif data.var == "day/night" then
		_G.DN_cycle = state
	end
end

function settings(button, name, dontChange, v)
	local cut,_ = string.gsub(name, "SETTINGS_", "")
	local data = Settings[cut]
	if data.type == "boolean" and button.canSet.Value == true then
		if not dontChange then
			local prev = settings_config[cut]
			settings_config[cut] = not settings_config[cut]
		end
		local state = v or settings_config[cut]
		temp_config_folder[cut].Value = state
		ReplicatedStorage.__config[data.var].Value = state
		if state then
			button.Text = "ON"
		else
			button.Text = "OFF"
		end
		settings_action(button, data, state)
	end
end 

for settingName,data in pairs(Settings) do
	if data.type == "boolean" then
		local frame = Storage.Miscs.settings_boolean:Clone()
		frame.setting_type.Text = settingName
		frame.desc.Text = data.description
		frame.desc.TextColor3 = data.desc_color
		frame.Name = settingName
		frame.Parent = UI.HUD.settingsFrame.list
		frame.LayoutOrder = data.order
		local button = frame.button
		button.Name = "SETTINGS_" .. settingName

		local k = temp_config_folder:FindFirstChild(settingName)
		if not k then
			k = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			k.Name = settingName
			k.Value = data.default
			k.Parent = temp_config_folder
		end

		local n = ReplicatedStorage.__config:FindFirstChild(data.var)
		if not n then
			n = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			n.Name = data.var
			n.Value = data.default
			n.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__config
		end

		settings_config[settingName] = k.Value
		button.Text = k.Value and "ON" or "OFF"

		handleButtonEvents(button)
		settings(button, button.Name, true, k.Value)
	end
end

local healthSaturation = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
healthSaturation.Parent = camera
local healthBlur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
healthBlur.Size = 0
healthBlur.Parent = camera

local inWater = false
local underwaterBlur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
underwaterBlur.Size = 0
underwaterBlur.Parent = camera
local underwaterSaturation = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
underwaterSaturation.Parent = camera

local damageTween
UI.hurtOverlay.ImageTransparency = 1
UI.hurtOverlay.Visible = true
UI.damageOverlay.Transparency = 1
UI.damageOverlay.Visible = true

function damageIndicator(part, dur)
	if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end

	local centreCF = getCentreCF(camera)
	local angleInDegrees = calculateAngle(centreCF, part.Position)

	local newIndicator = Storage.Miscs.damageIndicator:Clone()
	newIndicator.icon.ImageTransparency = 0
	newIndicator.Visible = true
	newIndicator.Rotation = angleInDegrees
	newIndicator.Parent = UI.indicators

	task.spawn(function()
		local lastPosition = part.Position
		while newIndicator:IsDescendantOf(game) do
			local centreCF = getCentreCF(camera)
			local angleInDegrees = calculateAngle(centreCF, lastPosition)

			newIndicator.Rotation = angleInDegrees

			if part:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				lastPosition = part.Position
			end
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end)

	task.wait(dur)
	local indicatorFadeTween = TweenService:Create(newIndicator.icon, TweenInfo.new(1), {ImageTransparency = 1})
	indicatorFadeTween:Play()
	indicatorFadeTween.Completed:Wait()

	newIndicator:Destroy()
end
Storage.Events.hitPos.OnClientEvent:Connect(damageIndicator)

local lastHealth = math.max(humanoid.Health - 15, 0)
local lastAttackerChar, lastAttacker
function updateHP(hp)
	hp = math.max(hp - 15, 0)
	local maxHealth = humanoid.MaxHealth - 15
	local damage = lastHealth - hp
	local alpha = clamp((hp / maxHealth) * 1, 0, 1)
	if not downed then
		TweenService:Create(UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.bar, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0), ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(188, 255, 196), alpha)}):Play()
	else
		TweenService:Create(UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.bar, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
	end
	TweenService:Create(UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.bar2, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 0.35), {Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {ImageTransparency = ((downed and 15 or math.max(hp, 15)) / (maxHealth * 0.8)) * 1, ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(68, 0, 3):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(138, 0, 5), alpha)}):Play()
	local size = math.max(1 - ((downed and 5 or math.max(hp, 5)) / (maxHealth / 1.8)), 0)
	TweenService:Create(healthSaturation, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Saturation = -size * 1.5, TintColor = downed and Color3.fromRGB(255, 98, 98) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Brightness = downed and -(0.5 - (hp / maxHealth) * 0.5) or 0}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(healthBlur, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Size = size * 10}):Play()
	UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.amount.Text = downed and "..." or math.abs(math.floor((hp / maxHealth) * 100))
	if damage > 1 then
		if humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnHit") then
			burned = tick()
		end
		local attacker, _, _, attackerCharacter = CombatTag:getKiller(character)
		if attacker and not lastAttackerChar and not lastAttacker and attackerCharacter then
			lastAttackerChar = attackerCharacter
			lastAttacker = attacker
		elseif attacker ~= lastAttacker then
			lastAttackerChar = attackerCharacter
			lastAttacker = attacker
		end

		if lastAttackerChar and not lastAttackerChar:IsDescendantOf(game) then
			lastAttackerChar = attackerCharacter
			Storage.Events.replay:Fire("str")
			Storage.Events.replay:Fire("clr")
		end

		Storage.Events.replay:Fire("sr")
		if damageTween then damageTween:Cancel() end
		UI.damageOverlay.Transparency =  math.max(1 - (damage / 150), 0.6)
		local length = math.clamp(damage / 12, 0.95, 1.35)
		damageTween = TweenService:Create(UI.damageOverlay, TweenInfo.new(length, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Transparency = 1})
		damageTween:Play()

		local saturation = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
		saturation.Brightness = 0.1
		saturation.Saturation = -0.5
		saturation.Parent = camera
		TweenService:Create(saturation, TweenInfo.new(length, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Saturation = 0, Brightness = 0}):Play()
		Debris:AddItem(saturation, length + 1)

		if humanoid.Health > 0 then
			local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
			blur.Size = damage
			blur.Parent = camera
			TweenService:Create(blur, TweenInfo.new(math.min(damage * 2 * 0.025, 3), Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = 0}):Play()
			Debris:AddItem(blur, 1.5)
		end

		local size = math.min(damage / 50, 1)
		local ran = {-size, size}
		size = ran[math.random(#ran)]
		SP_camBobbing:shove(Vector3.new(size / 2, size / 2, size))
		task.delay(0.15, function()
			SP_camBobbing:shove(Vector3.new(-(size / 2), -(size / 2), 0))
		end)
	end

	lastHealth = hp
end
task.spawn(function() updateHP(humanoid.Health) end)
table.insert(connections, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(updateHP))

Storage.Events.melee2.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
	damaged = tick()
end)

while #ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo:GetChildren() < 1 do
	task.wait()
end

function isUserOwnedGamepass(id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if table.find(data.id, tonumber(id)) and gamepasses:WaitForChild(name).Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

function getGamepassInstanceFromId(id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if table.find(data.id, tonumber(id)) then
			return gamepasses[name]
		end
	end

	return false
end

local loadoutType = {
	["Primary"] = true,
	["Second Primary"] = true,
	["Secondary"] = true,
	["Melee"] = true,
	["Utility"] = true,
	["Miscs"] = true,
}

local loadoutToNum = {
	["Primary"] = 1,
	["Second Primary"] = 2,
	["Secondary"] = 3,
	["Melee"] = 4,
	["Utility"] = 5,
	["Miscs"] = 6,
}

function updateLoadout(type)
	if player.playerData.loadout:FindFirstChild(loadoutToNum[type]) and ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo:FindFirstChild(type) then
		local itemName = player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[type]].Value
		if ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo[type]:FindFirstChild(itemName) then
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].itemName.Text = itemName
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].itemImage.Image = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo[type][itemName].textureId.Value
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].bg.Visible = true
		else
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].itemName.Text = "NONE"
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].itemImage.Image = ""
			UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].bg.Visible = false
		end
	end
end
for loadoutTypeString,_ in pairs(loadoutType) do
	updateLoadout(loadoutTypeString)
end

function updateLoadoutLists(type)
	if ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo:FindFirstChild(type) then
		for _,v in pairs(UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("ImageButton") and v ~= UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type] then
				v.UIStroke.Thickness = 2
				v.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(107, 107, 107)
			end
		end

		UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].UIStroke.Thickness = 2.5
		UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.loadout[type].UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

		for _,v in pairs(UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.weapons:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("ImageButton") then
				v:Destroy()
			end
		end

		for _,weapon in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo[type]:GetChildren()) do
			local button = Storage.Miscs.loadoutWeaponSlot:Clone()
			button.itemName.Text = weapon.Name
			button.itemImage.Image = weapon.textureId.Value
			if player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[type]].Value == weapon.Name then
				button.UIStroke.Thickness = 2.5
				button.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			end
			if weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") and not isUserOwnedGamepass(weapon.gamepassId.Value) then
				button.lock.Visible = true
				button.LayoutOrder = tick()

				local gamepassInstance = getGamepassInstanceFromId(weapon.gamepassId.Value)
				if gamepassInstance then
					local connections = {}
					table.insert(connections, gamepassInstance.Changed:Connect(function()
						button.lock.Visible = not gamepassInstance.Value
					end))

					table.insert(connections, button.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
						if parent == nil then
							for index,event in pairs(connections) do
								event:Disconnect()
								connections[index] = nil
							end

							connections = nil
						end
					end))
				end
			end
			button.Parent = UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.weapons
			handleButtonEvents(button)

			button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") and not isUserOwnedGamepass(weapon.gamepassId.Value) then
					local info = MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(weapon.gamepassId.Value, Enum.InfoType.GamePass)
					local prompt = promptMenu(2, {
						header = info.Name,
						header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 127),
						desc = info.Description,
						button1 = "CLOSE",
						button2 = "BUY",
					})
					if prompt == 2 then
						MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, weapon.gamepassId.Value)
					end

					return
				end

				local isUnequip = player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[type]].Value == weapon.Name
				for _,v in pairs(UI.HUD.loadoutFrame.main.weapons:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("ImageButton") then
						v.UIStroke.Thickness = 2
						v.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(107, 107, 107)
					end
				end

				local canUse = Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "setWeapon", {itemName = weapon.Name, loadoutType = type, unequip = isUnequip, weapon = weapon})
				if canUse then
					button.UIStroke.Thickness = isUnequip and 2 or 2.5
					button.UIStroke.Color = isUnequip and Color3.fromRGB(107, 107, 107) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

					updateLoadout(type)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end
updateLoadoutLists("Primary")

for _,armor in pairs(character.Armor:GetChildren()) do
	local connections = {}
	local armorFrame
	local function updateTierArmor()
		local newTier = armor:GetAttribute("Tier")
		if newTier ~= "" then
			if not armorFrame then
				armorFrame = Storage.Miscs.armorSlotFrame:Clone()
				armorFrame.Name = "TIER_" .. newTier .. "/" .. string.upper(armor.Name) .. "._vpf"
				armorFrame.Parent = UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.armorInfo.list

				local function updateArmorHP(hp)
					armor:SetAttribute("Health", math.min(hp, armor:GetAttribute("MaxHealth")))
					local alpha = clamp((hp / armor:GetAttribute("MaxHealth")) * 1, 0, 1)
					TweenService:Create(armorFrame.health.bar, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)}):Play()
				end
				updateArmorHP(armor:GetAttribute("Health"))

				table.insert(connections, armor:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
					updateArmorHP(armor:GetAttribute("Health"))
				end))

				local colorChanged = false
				table.insert(connections, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
					if armor:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 and armorFrame then
						colorChanged = true
						armorFrame.broken.Visible = true

						armorFrame.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 7):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(179, 0, 0), math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 2.5) * 0.5))
						armorFrame.broken.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(147, 6, 2):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(200, 65, 67), math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 2.5) * 0.5))
						armorFrame.health.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(223, 0, 0), math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 2.5) * 0.5))
					elseif armorFrame and colorChanged then
						colorChanged = false
						armorFrame.broken.Visible = false
						TweenService:Create(armorFrame.UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 7)}):Play()
						TweenService:Create(armorFrame.broken, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
						TweenService:Create(armorFrame.health, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)}):Play()
					end
				end))
			else
				for _,v in pairs(armorFrame:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Name == "_vpf" then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end

			local renderModel = Storage.Models.Armors[armor.Name]["TIER_" .. newTier]._vpf:Clone()
			renderModel.Parent = armorFrame
		end
	end
	updateTierArmor()
	armor:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Tier"):Connect(updateTierArmor)

	armor:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Equipped"):Connect(function()
		local equip = armor:GetAttribute("Equipped")
		if not equip and armorFrame then
			armorFrame:Destroy()
			armorFrame = nil

			for index,event in pairs(connections) do
				event:Disconnect()
				connections[index] = nil
			end
		end
	end)
end

task.spawn(function()
	while humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and humanoid.Health > 0 do
		TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(2, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), {Scale = 1.1}):Play()
		task.wait(1.5)
		TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Scale = 1}):Play()
		task.wait(1)
	end

	if humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
		TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Scale = 1}):Play()
	end
end)

CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("_flash"):Connect(function(object)
	if object:IsA("Sound") and (object.Parent:IsA("BasePart") or object.Parent:IsA("Attachment")) then
		local connections = {}
		local flashFX = UI.flash:Clone()
		flashFX.Name = object.Name .. "-flashFX"
		flashFX.Parent = UI

		table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local objectPos = object.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and object.Parent.Position or object.Parent:IsA("Attachment") and object.Parent.WorldPosition
			local distanceFactor = 1 - ((getDistance(camera.CFrame.p, objectPos) / object:GetAttribute("FlashDistance")) * 1)
			TweenService:Create(flashFX, TweenInfo.new(object:GetAttribute("FlashTime")), {BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (object.PlaybackLoudness / object:GetAttribute("FlashSoftness") * distanceFactor)}):Play()
		end))

		table.insert(connections, object.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
			if parent == nil then
				for index,event in pairs(connections) do
					event:Disconnect()
					connections[index] = nil
				end

				connections = nil

				TweenService:Create(flashFX, TweenInfo.new(1), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
				Debris:AddItem(flashFX, 1)
			end
		end))
	end
end)

local function sprint()
	if isMoving() and stamina.current > 0 and not _G.reload and (not _G.aim or _G.grenadeUse) and not _G.foodUse and tick() - _G.fireActive > (1 / 5) and not downed and not _G.meleeFinisher and not revive then
		stamina.elapsedTime = tick()
		stamina.state = true
		Network:FireServer("vars", "sprint", {state = "ye"})
		_G.isCrouch = false
		_G.isProne = false

		return true
	end

	return false
end

local backpackOpened = false
table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
	if not process then
		if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then
			while UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) do
				if sprint() then
					break
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			return
		elseif inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then
			while UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) do
				local tool = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
				if isAimable() and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and (not _G.reload or cameraMode == "TPS") and (cameraMode == "TPS" or humanoid.WalkSpeed <= game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed + 1) and not revive and _G.gunEquipped and (_G.mouseLock or _G.isFPS()) then
					_G.aim = true
					if not _G.grenadeEquipped and (tool and not tool:GetAttribute("NoShake") or not tool) then
						SP_aim.Speed = 2.5
						SP_aim:shove(aimEase)
					end
					if cameraMode == "FPS" and not _G.grenadeEquipped and (tool and not tool:GetAttribute("NoShake") or not tool) then
						playerGui.SOUNDS.aim0:Play()
					end

					break
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			return
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == cameraModeKey and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not downed then
			changeCameraMode(cameraMode == "TPS" and "FPS" or "TPS")
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space and stamina.current < 30 then
			UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.stamina.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
			TweenService:Create(UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.stamina.UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(125, 125, 125)}):Play()
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
			if lastSeller and lastSeller.Humanoid.Health > 0 and lastSeller.Humanoid.RootPart and not UI.HUD.cv_store.Visible then
				promptUI.prompt1.UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				TweenService:Create(promptUI.prompt1.UIStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.65, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)}):Play()
				closeOtherWindowsHUD(UI.HUD.cv_store)
				UI.HUD.cv_store.Visible = true
				HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
				UI.HUD.cv_store.UIScale.Scale = 0.8
				TweenService:Create(UI.HUD.cv_store.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic), {Scale = 1}):Play()
				playerGui.SOUNDS.ui_show:Play()
			elseif interactObject then
				--TweenService:Create(playerGui.PromptUI.prompt1.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Scale = 0.95}):Play()
				local object = interactObject:IsA("Model") and interactObject.PrimaryPart or interactObject
				local hold = interactObject.PromptData.HoldDuration > 0
				if hold then
					task.spawn(function()
						local currentObject = interactObject
						while UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) and interactObject and object and object:IsDescendantOf(game) and _G.onScreen(object.Position) and currentObject == interactObject do
							local interactSession = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.interact:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
							if interactSession then
								TweenService:Create(playerGui.PromptUI.prompt1.bar, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Size = UDim2.new((interactSession.currentTime.Value / interactSession.maxTime.Value) * 1, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
							else
								TweenService:Create(playerGui.PromptUI.prompt1.bar, TweenInfo.new(16e-5), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
							end

							task.wait()
						end

						TweenService:Create(playerGui.PromptUI.prompt1.bar, TweenInfo.new(16e-5), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
						Network:InvokeServer("interact", false)
					end)
				end

				local state, data = Network:InvokeServer("interact", interactObject)
				if data then
					_G.messageLog(data.prop, data.err, data.dur)
				end
			end
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not revive then
			local target = findClosetDownedPerson(nil, true)
			local melee = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
			if target and melee and melee:GetAttribute("Melee") then
				Storage.Events.melee:Fire(target)
			end
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
			local target = findClosetDownedPerson(5)
			local melee = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
			if target and melee and melee:GetAttribute("Melee") and melee.Name == "Fists" then
				local reviveState, reviveSession = Network:InvokeServer("revive", target)
				if reviveState then
					_G.isCrouch = false
					_G.isProne = false
					stamina.state = false
					revive = true
					revive_anim:Play(0.5)
					local progressFrame = UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.progress
					progressFrame.display.Text = "Recovering '" .. target.Name .. "'"
					progressFrame.Visible = true
					while reviveSession:IsDescendantOf(game) and character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") do
						if not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.R) then
							Network:InvokeServer("revive", false)
							break
						end
						progressFrame.display.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 5) * 0.5))
						progressFrame.bar.Size = UDim2.new((reviveSession.currentTime.Value / reviveSession.maxTime.Value) * 1, 0, 1, 0)
						task.wait()
					end
					progressFrame.Visible = false
					revive_anim:Stop(0.35)
					revive = false
				end
			end
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not _G.carry and not carry_db then
			local target = findClosetDownedPerson(3)
			local melee = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
			if target and melee and melee:GetAttribute("Melee") and melee.Name == "Fists" and target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and target.Humanoid.RootPart and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
				stamina.state = false
				_G.isCrouch = false
				_G.isProne = false
				carry_db = true
				local carryState, carrySession = Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "carry", {state = true, target = target})
				carry_db = false
				if carryState then
					humanoid:UnequipTools()
					carry_anim.idle:Play()
					_G.carry = true
					local up, up2 = false, false
					local time = tick()
					while carrySession and carrySession:IsDescendantOf(game) do
						if not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.G) and not up then
							up = true
						end
						if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.G) and up then
							up2 = true
							if tick() - time > 0.5 then
								Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "carry", {throw = true})
								break
							end
						elseif not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.G) and up and up2 then
							time = tick()

							Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "carry", {})
							break
						end

						if not up or not up2 then
							time = tick()
						end

						if _G.isProne or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") then
							Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "carry", {})
							break
						end

						task.wait()
					end
					_G.carry = false
					carry_anim.idle:Stop()
				end
			end
	      elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.P then
			backpackOpened = not backpackOpened
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.C and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and not downed and not _G.meleeFinisher and not revive and not _G.isProne then
			_G.isCrouch = true

			_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.prone, humanoid.RootPart, nil, nil, 0.4):Play()
			if stamina.state and tick() - stamina.elapsedTime >= 0.3 and isMovingForward() and stamina.current > 10 then
				stamina.current = math.clamp(stamina.current - 10, 0, stamina.max)
				stamina.t = tick()

				slide_anim:Play(0.25)
				local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 0, 1e5)
				bodyVelocity.Velocity = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.LookVector * 45
				bodyVelocity.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
				TweenService:Create(bodyVelocity, TweenInfo.new(2.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {Velocity = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.LookVector * 2.5}):Play()
				local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				if torso then
					torso.CollisionGroup = "NoCollisions"
				end
				humanoid.RootPart.CanCollide = true
				_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.slide.start, humanoid.RootPart):Play()
				local actionSFX = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.slide.action, humanoid.RootPart)
				actionSFX:Play()
				while _G.isCrouch do
					if bodyVelocity.Velocity.magnitude <= 5 then
						break
					end

					task.wait(1 / 30)
				end

				local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				if torso then
					torso.CollisionGroup = humanoid.Health > 0 and "Alive" or "Death"
				end
				humanoid.RootPart.CanCollide = false

				TweenService:Create(actionSFX, TweenInfo.new(1), {Volume = 0}):Play()
				_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.slide.stop, humanoid.RootPart):Play()
				slide_anim:Stop(0.45)
				bodyVelocity:Destroy()

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
					sprint()
				end
			end
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.X and not _G.isCrouch and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and not downed and not _G.meleeFinisher then
			if _G.isCrouch or _G.isProne then
				_G.isCrouch = false
				_G.isProne = false
			else
				_G.isCrouch = false
				_G.isProne = true
			end

			_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.prone, humanoid.RootPart, nil, nil, 0.4):Play()
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Y and UI:FindFirstChild("blood_PX") and not _G.reload then
			local wipeAnim = loadAnim(15853386392)
			wipeAnim:Play()
			playerGui.SOUNDS.wipe:Play()

			local bloodFrames = {}
			for _,v in pairs(UI:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "blood_PX" then
					v.Name = "clean"
					bloodFrames[#bloodFrames + 1] = v
				end
			end

			task.wait(wipeAnim.Length / 2)
			for _,v in pairs(bloodFrames) do
				TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {ImageTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, v.Position.X.Offset + -50, 0, v.Position.Y.Offset + -50)}):Play()
				Debris:AddItem(v, 1)
			end
		elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl then
			_G.mouseLock = not _G.mouseLock
		end
	end
end))

table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
	if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and _G.aim then
		_G.aimTick = tick()
		_G.aim = false
		local tool = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
		if not _G.grenadeEquipped and (tool and not tool:GetAttribute("NoShake") or not tool) then
			SP_aim:shove(aimEase)
		end
		isActuallyAiming = false
		if cameraMode == "FPS" and not _G.grenadeEquipped and (tool and not tool:GetAttribute("NoShake") or not tool) then
			playerGui.SOUNDS.aim1:Play()
		end
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then
		stamina.state = false
		Network:FireServer("vars", "sprint", {state = "nah"})
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.C then
		_G.isCrouch = false

		_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.prone, humanoid.RootPart, nil, nil, 0.4):Play()
	elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
		--TweenService:Create(playerGui.PromptUI.prompt1.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {Scale = 1}):Play()
	end
end))

camera.FieldOfView = 70
local isDead = false
function dead()
	if isDead then
		return
	end

	for _,v in pairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("_Trajectory")) do
		CollectionService:RemoveTag(v, "_Trajectory")
		v:Destroy()
	end
	isDead = true
	changeCameraMode("TPS")
	UserInputService.MouseDeltaSensitivity = defMouseSense

	if connections then
		for index,event in pairs(connections) do
			event:Disconnect()
			connections[index] = nil
		end
		connections = nil
	end

	humanoid.HipHeight = 0
	if downedShake then
		cameraShake:stop(downedShake)
		downedShake = nil
	end

	if downedConnection then
		downedConnection:Disconnect()
		downedConnection = nil
	end

	HUD_GAMEPLAY(false)
	UI.Cursor.Visible = false
	StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, false)
	local startTime = tick()
	local respawnTime = 3

	local waitTick = tick()
	while player.CameraMinZoomDistance < 7 and tick() - waitTick < 1 do
		task.wait()
	end

	local deathFrame = UI.deathFrame
	deathFrame.main.icon.ImageTransparency = 1
	deathFrame.icon_prime.UIScale.Scale = 2
	TweenService:Create(healthSaturation, TweenInfo.new(2.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Brightness = 0, Saturation = 0}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(deathFrame.main.icon, TweenInfo.new(3.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 2.5), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(deathFrame.icon_prime, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Circular), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(deathFrame.icon_prime.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Circular), {Scale = 1}):Play()
	for i,v in pairs(deathFrame.main:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
			v.TextTransparency = 1
			task.delay((i / 2) + 1.5, function()
				TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(3.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {TextTransparency = 0, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5}):Play()
			end)
		end
	end
	deathFrame.main.quote.Text = DeathQuotes[math.random(#DeathQuotes)]
	deathFrame.Visible = true

	local cameraCF = camera.CFrame
	local deathFX = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
	deathFX.Contrast = -5
	deathFX.Saturation = 2.5
	deathFX.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(85, 0, 0)
	deathFX.Parent = camera
	local deathBlur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
	deathBlur.Size = 0
	deathBlur.Parent = camera
	TweenService:Create(deathFX, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Circular), {TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 73), Saturation = -1, Contrast = 0}):Play()
	--TweenService:Create(deathFX, TweenInfo.new(3.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 2.5), {Contrast = 0.35}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(deathBlur, TweenInfo.new(2.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 2.5), {Size = 5}):Play()
	--TweenService:Create(UI.fadeOverlay, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, 0, false, 2.5), {Transparency = 0}):Play()
	TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {ImageTransparency = 0.15}):Play()
	task.delay(2.5, function()
		TweenService:Create(UI.hurtOverlay, TweenInfo.new(3.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {ImageTransparency = 0.25}):Play()
		Storage.Events.replay:Fire("str")
	end)

	local replayAccess = false
	task.delay(1, function()
		deathFrame.panel.Visible = true
		deathFrame.panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1.05, 0)
		TweenService:Create(deathFrame.panel, TweenInfo.new(2.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
		task.wait(1.5)
		replayAccess = true
	end)

	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	if humanoid.RootPart and character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
		camera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(math.random(-5, 5)))
	end

	_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.cinematic_boom1, playerGui):Play()
	local springCam = Spring.new()
	local boom = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.cinematic_boom, playerGui)
	if boom then
		boom:Play()
	end

	local replayAvailable = false
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
		if not process then
			if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
				respawnFunction(deathFrame.panel.respawn.respawnButton)
			elseif inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q and replayAccess and replayAvailable then
				replayFunction(deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton)
			end
		end
	end)

	TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(5), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()
	local killerName, killerDistance
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
		local newDt = dt * 60

		if killerPlayer ~= nil and killerPlayer ~= false and not killerName and humanoid.RootPart then
			killerName = killerPlayer.Name
			if humanoid.RootPart and killerPlayer and killerPlayer.Character.Humanoid.RootPart then
				killerDistance = getDistance(killerPlayer.Character.Humanoid.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart)
			end
		end

		local respawnTimeLeft = math.ceil(respawnTime - (tick() - startTime))
		deathFrame.main.respawn.Text = respawnTimeLeft > -1 and "RESPAWN AVAILABLE IN " .. math.abs(respawnTimeLeft) or killerName and "KILLED BY '" .. string.upper(killerName) .. "' [" .. (killerDistance and math.ceil(killerDistance) or "?") .. " STUDS]" or "KILLED BY 'YOU'"
		if respawnTimeLeft <= -1 then
			deathFrame.main.respawn.TextSize = 15
			deathRespawn = true
		end

		if not deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton:FindFirstChild("noReplay") then
			if killerPlayer == nil then
				deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton.Text = "LOADING REPLAY"
			elseif killerPlayer == false and replayAvailable then
				deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton.Text = "REPLAY UNAVAILABLE"
			elseif replayAvailable then
				deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton.Text = "VIEW REPLAY [Q]"
				deathFrame.panel.replay.fade.Visible = false	
			else
				deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton.Text = "REPLAY DISABLED"
				deathFrame.panel.replay.fade.Visible = false
			end
		else
			deathFrame.panel.replay.replayButton.Text = replayAvailable and "REPLAY UNAVAILABLE" or "REPLAY DISABLED"
			deathFrame.panel.replay.fade.Visible = false
		end

		deathFrame.panel.respawn.respawnButton.Text = not deathRespawn and "RESPAWN UNAVAILABLE" or "RESPAWN [SPACE]"
		deathFrame.panel.respawn.fade.Visible = not deathRespawn

		if character:FindFirstChild("Head") and humanoid.RootPart and not replaying then
			local boomLoudness = boom.PlaybackLoudness
			_G.deathTween = TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(1), {CFrame = CFrame.new(clampDistanceBetweenVectors(character.Head.CFrame.Position, cameraCF.Position, 10), character.Head.CFrame.Position)})
			_G.deathTween:Play()

			springCam:shove(Vector3.new(
				math.random(-boomLoudness, boomLoudness) / 30000,
				math.random(-boomLoudness, boomLoudness) / 30000,
				math.random(-boomLoudness, boomLoudness) / 30000)
			)
			local v3 = springCam:update(dt)
			camera.CFrame *= CFrame.Angles(v3.x * newDt, v3.y * newDt, v3.z * newDt)
		end
	end)
end

humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_,state)
	if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
		stamina.t = tick()
		stamina.current = math.max(stamina.current - 5, 0)
		humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
		task.wait(1.5)
		while stamina.current <= 10 do
			task.wait(1 / 15)
		end
		humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
	elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
		if not falling then
			falling = true

			local lastDamagePos = humanoid.RootPart.Position
			local lastPos = humanoid.RootPart.Position
			local elaspedTime = 0
			local lastHitPos
			while humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming and humanoid.RootPart do
				local dt = RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
				elaspedTime += dt
				local currentPos = humanoid.RootPart.Position
				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character, workspace.CurrentCamera, workspace.Ignored}
				local landedRay = workspace:Raycast(humanoid.RootPart.Position, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.unit * math.clamp(5 * (dt * 60), 5, 20), params)
				if landedRay and lastPos and (lastPos - currentPos).magnitude >= 20 and not isInWater(4, humanoid.RootPart) and landedRay.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water and (not lastHitPos or getDistance(lastHitPos, humanoid.RootPart) >= 5) then
					local damage = (lastPos - currentPos).magnitude * elaspedTime
					if downed then
						damage *= 3.5
					end

					Network:FireServer("vars", "fall", {damage = damage})
					lastDamagePos = currentPos

					lastHitPos = landedRay.Position
					elaspedTime = 0
				end
			end

			falling = false
		end
	elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing then
		_G.isCrouch = false
		_G.isProne = false
	elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
		Network:FireServer("vars", "dead")
		dead()
	end
end)

table.insert(connections, humanoid.Running:Connect(function(speed)
	characterSpeed = speed
end))

Storage.Events.killer.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(type)
	killerPlayer = type
end)

Storage.Events.shake.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
	if not replaying then
		cameraShake:shake(...)
	end
end)

Storage.Events.shake_client.Event:Connect(function(...)
	if not replaying then
		cameraShake:shake(...)
	end
end)

Storage.Events.die.OnClientEvent:Connect(dead)

Storage.Events.client.Event:Connect(function(type, ...)
	if type == "cGunFire" then
		local tool, settings = unpack({...})
		Crosshair:Shove(Crosshair.Radius * settings.crosshairShoveSize)
		SP_recoil.Speed = settings.vmRecoilSpeed or 10
		SP_recoil:shove(settings.vmRecoil or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1.5))

		if tool:GetAttribute("RateHeat") then
			SP_recoilANG.Damping = 2.5
			SP_recoilANG:shove(Vector3.new(
				math.random(-50, 50) / 1000,
				math.random(-50, 50) / 1000,
				0)
					* math.max(tool:GetAttribute("RateHeat"), 0.25)
			)
		end
	elseif type == "cMeleeFire" then
		local tool, settings = unpack({...})
		Crosshair:Shove(Crosshair.Radius * settings.crosshairShoveSize)
	elseif type == "crosshair" then
		local enable, radius = unpack({...})
		if enable and not Crosshair.Enabled then
			Crosshair:Enable(radius)
		elseif not enable and Crosshair.Enabled then
			Crosshair:Disable()
		end
	elseif type == "setCrosshair" then
		Crosshair:Set(...)
	end
end)

function downedWeapon()
	local secondaryGun
	for _,v in pairs(player.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if v:GetAttribute("Arms") and v:GetAttribute("Secondary") then
			secondaryGun = v
			break
		end
	end
	if secondaryGun then
		humanoid:EquipTool(secondaryGun)
	end
end

local wasFPS = false
table.insert(connections, humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdoll"):Connect(function()
	local state = humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
	if cameraMode == "FPS" and state then
		wasFPS = true
		changeCameraMode("TPS")
	elseif wasFPS and not state and not downed then
		wasFPS = false
		changeCameraMode("FPS")
	end

	if state then
		_G.isCrouch = false
		_G.isProne = false
	else
		if downed then
			downedWeapon()
		end
	end

	local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	if torso then
		if downed and state then
			torso.CollisionGroup = humanoid.Health > 0 and "Alive" or "Death"
		elseif downed and not state then
			torso.CollisionGroup = "NoCollisions"
		end
	end
end))

					if selfReviveState then
						while selfReviveSession:IsDescendantOf(game) do
							if not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then
								Network:InvokeServer("varsFunction", "selfRevive", {})

							task.wait()
						end
					end

					TweenService:Create(downedFrame.selfHeal.UIScale, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Scale = 1}):Play()
					TweenService:Create(downedFrame.selfHeal.key, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(226, 226, 226)}):Play()
					downedFrame.selfHeal.bar.UIGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)

					bleedout = false
				end
			end
		end)

			while downed and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart do
				local reviveSFX = humanoid.RootPart:FindFirstChild("reviveSFX")
				if reviveSFX then
					downedFrame.recover.Text = [[']] .. reviveSFX:GetAttribute("reviver") .. [[' is recovering you...]]
					TweenService:Create(downedFrame.recover, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {TextTransparency = 0, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5}):Play()
				else
					TweenService:Create(downedFrame.recover, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1}):Play()
				end

				task.wait(1 / 15)
			end
		end)
	else
		UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.display.Text = "HEALTH"
		UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.health.amount.Text = math.abs(math.floor((math.max(humanoid.Health - 15, 0) / 75) * 100))
		if downedShake then
			cameraShake:stop(downedShake)
			downedShake = nil
		end

		if downedConnection then
			downedConnection:Disconnect()
			downedConnection = nil
		end
	end
